---
title: "Error codes"
description: "Every API refusal and source reason, grouped by product area, with its HTTP status, meaning, what to do and whether to retry."
---

{/* Generated by scripts/sync-contract.mjs from tetrafi-rfq docs/api/integrator/error-codes.json. Edit the source, then run npm run sync. */}

Find the code from the `error` field of the response: its `docUrl` links straight to its entry and opens its area below. To parse the error body and retry safely, read [Errors and refusals](/trade/errors).

<Note>New codes and reasons can appear at any time. Handle one you do not recognise by its HTTP status ([Codes you do not know](/trade/errors#codes-you-do-not-know)).</Note>

## Codes you will meet first

- [`AUTH_REQUIRED`](#AUTH_REQUIRED) · 401<br />Not authenticated. Act on `details.reason`: send a valid key, or fix the signature it names ([Authentication](/authentication)).
- [`WORKSPACE_REQUIRED`](#WORKSPACE_REQUIRED) · 400<br />A session, or a key bound to no workspace, sent no `X-Workspace-Id`. Send it.
- [`INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE`](#INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE) · 403<br />Use a key that holds `details.requiredScope` ([Permissions](/authentication#permissions)).
- [`INSUFFICIENT_PERMISSION`](#INSUFFICIENT_PERMISSION) · 403<br />The key's owner no longer holds the permission `details.permission` names. Ask a workspace admin.
- [`IP_NOT_ALLOWED`](#IP_NOT_ALLOWED) · 403<br />Call from an address on the key's IP allowlist, or update the allowlist.
- [`STEP_UP_REQUIRED`](#STEP_UP_REQUIRED) · 403<br />Signed-in members only; API keys never get it. Complete the step-up `details.stepUpSubject` names, then retry with its proof in the `X-Step-Up-Proof` header.
- [`INVALID_REQUEST`](#INVALID_REQUEST) · 400<br />Correct the request as `message` and `details` say, then send it again.
- [`RATE_LIMITED`](#RATE_LIMITED) · 429<br />Wait `Retry-After` seconds, then retry ([Rate limits](/reference/rate-limits)).
- [`QUOTE_NOT_FOUND`](#QUOTE_NOT_FOUND) · 404<br />No quote with this id is available to you. Check the id, or request a new quote.
- [`QUOTE_EXPIRED`](#QUOTE_EXPIRED) · 410<br />Past `expiresAt`; `next` is `requote`: [requote it](/search/firmness-and-lifetime#refresh-an-expired-quote).
- [`QUOTE_NOT_HELD`](#QUOTE_NOT_HELD) · 409<br />The quote is no longer held, so nothing was sent: [requote it](/search/firmness-and-lifetime#requote-a-quote).
- [`MIN_RECEIVE_REQUIRED`](#MIN_RECEIVE_REQUIRED) · 400<br />The quote card's `minReceiveRequired` is true: set `minReceive` on the order.
- [`INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE`](#INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE) · 422<br />Add funds or lower the amount, then place the order again.
- [`IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED`](#IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED) · 400<br />Send an `Idempotency-Key` header with every `POST /api/v1/orders`.
- [`IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT`](#IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT) · 409<br />The key was used for a different body. Fix your code, then [recover by quote](/trade/orders#recover-an-order-by-its-quote).
- [`IDEMPOTENCY_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`](#IDEMPOTENCY_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN) · 409<br />The first request may or may not have taken effect. Stop sending this key and read the resource back (`details.readTarget`).
- [`ORDER_IN_PROGRESS`](#ORDER_IN_PROGRESS) · 409<br />An order with this key is still being placed. Wait `Retry-After`, then read the order.
- [`QUOTE_ALREADY_ORDERED`](#QUOTE_ALREADY_ORDERED) · 409<br />Another order already used this quote. It is not a failure: [recover it by its quote](/trade/orders#recover-an-order-by-its-quote).
- [`ORDER_NOT_FOUND`](#ORDER_NOT_FOUND) · 404<br />Check the order id and the workspace.
- [`SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE`](#SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE) · 503<br />It may not have completed. Retry a read with backoff; read a write back first ([Retries and recovery](/trade/orders#retries-and-recovery)).

## Refusal codes

Act on each code's own **Do** line and **Retry** answer, not on its status alone: codes with one status can need different steps. On an order, a timeout or `5xx` leaves the outcome unknown until you [recover the order by its quote](/trade/orders#retries-and-recovery).

<Accordion title="Requests, keys and access">

Malformed requests, credentials, request signing, permissions, workspaces, idempotency, paging and batch reads. 114 codes.

<span id="ACCOUNT_KEY_AUTHORITY_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ACCOUNT_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The request needs a current API key owned by the account with the required permission.<br />**Do:** Use a current account-owned key that holds the permission, or ask an admin to grant it.

<span id="API_KEY_ID_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>API_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />ID_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The apiKeyId filter is empty or carries surrounding whitespace.<br />**Do:** Send the API key id exactly, or leave the filter out.

<span id="API_KEY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>API_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The request's API key, or its request-signing state, could not be identified or confirmed as current.<br />**Do:** Retry shortly; if it persists, check the key is still active.

<span id="API_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>API_<wbr />VERSION_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The TetraFi-Version header is not exactly one supported version date (YYYY-MM-DD); nothing ran.<br />**Do:** Send a supported version (the message lists them), or omit the header to use your API key's pinned version.

<span id="AUTHORIZATION_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>AUTHORIZATION_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Current workspace access could not be verified.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>AUTH_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CONFIGURED</code> · 500 · Retry: no<br />Authentication is enabled but the token service is not configured on this server.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="AUTH_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>AUTH_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 401 · Retry: no<br />This operation needs an authenticated caller, or the presented credential was refused. details.reason names the cause (missingCredentials when no credential was sent) and details.remediation its fix: sendCredentials, useActiveKey, issueNewKey, issueWorkspaceKey, requestNewToken or signRequest.<br />**Do:** Act on details.reason. Absent or missingCredentials: send an API key (X-API-Key) or a bearer token. invalidApiKey: the key is unknown or no longer active; use an active key. apiKeyExpired: the key has expired; issue a new key. legacyUserKeyRequiresRebinding: the key has no workspace binding; issue a workspace key. serviceAccountCredentialRevoked: the key that issued this bearer token is no longer active; request a token with an active key. invalidToken: the bearer token is malformed or not valid here; request a new token. tokenExpired: the bearer token has expired; request a new token. sessionRevoked: the token's session was revoked; request a new token. signatureRequired: this key signs its requests; send X-Timestamp and X-Signature. signatureInvalid: a signing header is malformed or the signature does not match; check the canonical string and the signing secret. timestampOutOfRange: X-Timestamp is more than 300 seconds from server time, or more than 10 seconds ahead on a request with X-Nonce; correct the clock and sign again. nonceRequired: a signed POST, PUT, PATCH or DELETE needs X-Nonce; sign again with one. nonceInvalid: send X-Nonce once, as 16 to 128 characters of A-Z, a-z, 0-9, - or _. signatureReplayed: this nonce was already accepted for this key; sign again with a new nonce. signaturePredatesRestart: reserved, not answered today; if it is, sign again with a fresh timestamp and nonce.

<span id="BATCH_EXCEEDS_RATE_BURST" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BATCH_<wbr />EXCEEDS_<wbr />RATE_<wbr />BURST</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The credential's rate limit admits fewer requests at once than the batch holds, so waiting would not help; nothing ran.<br />**Do:** Send a smaller batch.

<span id="BATCH_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BATCH_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The batch body is malformed, empty, or has an item without a unique 1 to 64 character id or with a url that is not a path and query; details.itemId names the item when one is at fault.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="BATCH_ITEM_NOT_BATCHABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BATCH_<wbr />ITEM_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />BATCHABLE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />An item reads a stream, an export or a file, which cannot be batched; details.itemId names the item.<br />**Do:** Read it as a separate request.

<span id="BATCH_ITEM_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BATCH_<wbr />ITEM_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · 502 · Retry: no<br />Answered as one item's body inside a 200 batch: that item's answer could not be read. The item keeps its own status when it was a refusal, else 502.<br />**Do:** Send that read as a separate request.

<span id="BATCH_METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BATCH_<wbr />METHOD_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ALLOWED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />A batch item can only be a GET; details.itemId names the item.<br />**Do:** Send writes as separate requests.

<span id="BATCH_RELATED_READ_BUDGET_EXCEEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BATCH_<wbr />RELATED_<wbr />READ_<wbr />BUDGET_<wbr />EXCEEDED</code> · 429 · Retry: no<br />Answered as one item's status and body inside a 200 batch: that read's related reads do not fit what is left of the batch's read allowance, so it did not run.<br />**Do:** Send that read as a separate request.

<span id="BATCH_RESPONSE_TOO_LARGE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BATCH_<wbr />RESPONSE_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />LARGE</code> · 413 · Retry: no<br />Answered as one item's status and body inside a 200 batch: the batch answer reached its 8 MiB limit before this item.<br />**Do:** Read that item as a separate request, or batch fewer large reads.

<span id="BATCH_SIGNED_REQUEST_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BATCH_<wbr />SIGNED_<wbr />REQUEST_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />A signed API-key request cannot be batched: each read would need its own signature.<br />**Do:** Send the reads as separate signed requests.

<span id="BATCH_TOO_MANY_ITEMS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BATCH_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />MANY_<wbr />ITEMS</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />A batch holds at most 20 items.<br />**Do:** Split the reads across several batches.

<span id="BATCH_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BATCH_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Batches cannot be admitted just now; nothing ran.<br />**Do:** Retry shortly, or send the reads separately.

<span id="BATCH_UNKNOWN_ROUTE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BATCH_<wbr />UNKNOWN_<wbr />ROUTE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />An item url is not a GET operation of the API reference; details.itemId names the item.<br />**Do:** Correct the item's path, or read it as a separate request.

<span id="CATEGORY_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CATEGORY_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The category filter is not one of direct, sub_workspace or affiliated.<br />**Do:** Send a listed category, or leave the filter out.

<span id="CHANNEL_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CHANNEL_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The channel filter is not one of ui, api, whitelabel or embedded.<br />**Do:** Send a listed channel, or leave the filter out.

<span id="CREDENTIAL_CORE_NOT_WIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDENTIAL_<wbr />CORE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />WIRED</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />This server process cannot run venue-account operations yet.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="CREDENTIAL_RESEAL_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDENTIAL_<wbr />RESEAL_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The stored venue credential cannot be used: a legacy LP key uses retired key storage, or the venue account's key is not sealed with the current key.<br />**Do:** For a legacy LP key, connect a new key for the venue; for a venue account's key, rotate it. Then try again.

<span id="CURSOR_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CURSOR_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The cursor is not one this API issued.<br />**Do:** Start the listing again without a cursor.

<span id="CURSOR_STALE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CURSOR_<wbr />STALE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The list changed since this cursor was issued.<br />**Do:** Start the listing again without a cursor.

<span id="EMAIL_VERIFICATION_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>EMAIL_<wbr />VERIFICATION_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The signed-in person has not verified their email.<br />**Do:** Verify the email address, then retry.

<span id="EXPORT_TOO_LARGE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>EXPORT_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />LARGE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The export would hold more rows than one file allows.<br />**Do:** Narrow the filters or the date range, then export again.

<span id="FORBIDDEN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FORBIDDEN</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The caller may not read this record.<br />**Do:** Use a credential whose role covers the record.

<span id="IDEMPOTENCY_AUTHORITY_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>IDEMPOTENCY_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The caller no longer holds the authority needed to replay this stored response.<br />**Do:** Use a credential with the same authority as the original request.

<span id="IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>IDEMPOTENCY_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This Idempotency-Key was already used with a different request body.<br />**Do:** Use a new Idempotency-Key for a different request.

<span id="IDEMPOTENCY_IN_FLIGHT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>IDEMPOTENCY_<wbr />IN_<wbr />FLIGHT</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />An earlier request with this Idempotency-Key is still running.<br />**Do:** Wait, then send the same request with the same Idempotency-Key again.

<span id="IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>IDEMPOTENCY_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The Idempotency-Key header is repeated, padded, empty or too long.<br />**Do:** Send one Idempotency-Key of 1 to 255 characters.

<span id="IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>IDEMPOTENCY_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This operation requires an Idempotency-Key header.<br />**Do:** Add an Idempotency-Key header and send the request again.

<span id="IDEMPOTENCY_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>IDEMPOTENCY_<wbr />OUTCOME_<wbr />UNKNOWN</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The earlier request with this Idempotency-Key may or may not have taken effect; retrying the key cannot tell.<br />**Do:** Stop retrying this key. Read the resource back (details.readTarget names the read where the operation has one) and send a new Idempotency-Key only once that read shows nothing happened.

<span id="IDEMPOTENCY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>IDEMPOTENCY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The request's idempotency record could not be verified.<br />**Do:** Retry with the same Idempotency-Key shortly.

<span id="IDEMPOTENT_ANSWER_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>IDEMPOTENT_<wbr />ANSWER_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 500 · Retry: no<br />The request ran, but its answer could not be read.<br />**Do:** Read the resource before retrying; never resend it with a new Idempotency-Key.

<span id="IDEMPOTENT_SECRET_ALREADY_SHOWN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>IDEMPOTENT_<wbr />SECRET_<wbr />ALREADY_<wbr />SHOWN</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This Idempotency-Key already created the resource named by details.resourceId; its secret was shown once and is not stored.<br />**Do:** Use the secret from the first answer, or rotate or reissue the resource named by details.resourceId.

<span id="INCLUDE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INCLUDE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The include parameter is malformed, repeated or names too many relations.<br />**Do:** List the relation names once, separated by commas, as the message says, then send the request again.

<span id="INCLUDE_SIGNED_REQUEST_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INCLUDE_<wbr />SIGNED_<wbr />REQUEST_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />include is not available on a signed API-key request.<br />**Do:** Read the related resources with their own requests.

<span id="INCLUDE_TARGET_LIMIT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INCLUDE_<wbr />TARGET_<wbr />LIMIT</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />An included entry was not read because the answer already holds as many related objects as one request may include.<br />**Do:** Read the remaining related resources with their own requests.

<span id="INCLUDE_TARGET_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INCLUDE_<wbr />TARGET_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · 502 · Retry: yes<br />An included related object could not be read; the entry's status names the read's answer.<br />**Do:** Read that related resource with its own request, or retry shortly.

<span id="INCLUDE_TOO_DEEP" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INCLUDE_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />DEEP</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />An include path is nested more levels deep than the API allows.<br />**Do:** Shorten the include path, or read the related resource separately.

<span id="INCLUDE_TOO_LARGE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INCLUDE_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />LARGE</code> · 413 · Retry: no<br />An included entry was left out because the answer would exceed its size limit.<br />**Do:** Read that related resource with its own request.

<span id="INCLUDE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INCLUDE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Related objects cannot be expanded on this request.<br />**Do:** Read the related resources separately, or retry shortly.

<span id="INCLUDE_UNKNOWN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INCLUDE_<wbr />UNKNOWN</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The include parameter names a relation this resource does not have; the message lists the allowed ones.<br />**Do:** Use only the relations the message lists, then send the request again.

<span id="INSUFFICIENT_PERMISSION" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INSUFFICIENT_<wbr />PERMISSION</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The caller's role, key or credential does not allow this action; details.permission names the permission it needs when one is known (details.anyOf when any of several will do).<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin for the permission details.permission names.

<span id="INSUFFICIENT_SCOPE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INSUFFICIENT_<wbr />SCOPE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The API key lacks the scope this operation needs; details.requiredScope names it. details.reason names the cause (keyLacksScope, keyUnavailable, tokenLacksScope; keyOwnerPermissionWithdrawn when the key's creator no longer has the workspace permission behind it; serviceAccountPermissionMissing when the service account's own role no longer allows it) and details.remediation its fix (useKeyWithScope, requestTokenWithScope, restoreKeyOwnerPermission or restoreServiceAccountPermission).<br />**Do:** Use a key, or request a token, that holds details.requiredScope; when the key's creator or the service account lost the permission, a workspace admin restores it.

<span id="INTEGRATION_APP_FORBIDDEN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INTEGRATION_<wbr />APP_<wbr />FORBIDDEN</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The integration app is not allowed to use this quote or order.<br />**Do:** Use the integration app the quote was requested with.

<span id="INTEGRATION_APP_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INTEGRATION_<wbr />APP_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The request's integration app differs from the quote's.<br />**Do:** Use the integration app the quote was requested with.

<span id="INTEGRATION_APP_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INTEGRATION_<wbr />APP_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The integration app binding could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="INVALID_REQUEST" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INVALID_<wbr />REQUEST</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The request could not be read or has the wrong shape; details.field names the path of a missing required, unknown, mistyped or out-of-range field. A malformed body without a field path omits details.field. details.reason names the cause (fieldInvalid with a field path; bodyUnreadable when the body could not be read; else requestInvalid; unless the refusing step names its own) and details.remediation the fix (correctField, correctBody or correctRequest).<br />**Do:** Correct the request as the message and details say, then send it again.

<span id="IP_NOT_ALLOWED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>IP_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ALLOWED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The API key is restricted to an IP allowlist that does not include this caller.<br />**Do:** Call from an allowed IP, or update the key's allowlist.

<span id="JWT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>JWT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This action needs a signed-in person, not an API key.<br />**Do:** Have a member perform it in a signed-in session.

<span id="KEY_ACCESS_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>KEY_<wbr />ACCESS_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The API key's access changed since the request was admitted.<br />**Do:** Retry the request so it is admitted under the key's current access.

<span id="KEY_AUTHORITY_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>KEY_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The request needs a current workspace key with the required permission.<br />**Do:** Use a current key that holds the permission.

<span id="KEY_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>KEY_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The API key's current authority could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="KEY_IP_EVIDENCE_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>KEY_<wbr />IP_<wbr />EVIDENCE_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The key's verified IP evidence has expired; this read does not renew it.<br />**Do:** Verify the account's key to renew its evidence, then read again.

<span id="LIMIT_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LIMIT_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />limit is outside 1 to 100.<br />**Do:** Send a limit from 1 to 100, or omit it for the default page size.

<span id="METADATA_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>METADATA_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The request's metadata is not valid: an order's metadata must be a JSON object.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>METHOD_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ALLOWED</code> · 405 · Retry: no<br />This HTTP method is not allowed on this path.<br />**Do:** Use the method the API reference lists for this path.

<span id="MFA_ENROLMENT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>MFA_<wbr />ENROLMENT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The workspace requires a second factor for money actions, and the member has none enrolled.<br />**Do:** Enrol an authenticator for the member, then retry.

<span id="MFA_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>MFA_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This action needs a session that completed its second factor.<br />**Do:** Complete the second factor, then retry.

<span id="MFA_REQUIRED_BY_WORKSPACE_POLICY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>MFA_<wbr />REQUIRED_<wbr />BY_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />POLICY</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The workspace's MFA policy requires this signed-in member to verify a second factor, and the grace period has ended.<br />**Do:** With a second factor already set up, sign out and sign in again with it; without one, set one up in the TetraFi app (Security), then sign in again with it. The refusal carries refusalFix setUpTwoFactor only when TetraFi read that the member has none.

<span id="MFA_STALE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>MFA_<wbr />STALE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />Trading needs a second factor confirmed within the last 12 hours; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Confirm the second factor again, then retry.

<span id="NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />Nothing exists at this path, or it is not visible to the caller.<br />**Do:** Check the path and id.

<span id="NOT_SHOWN_TO_YOU" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>NOT_<wbr />SHOWN_<wbr />TO_<wbr />YOU</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The included entry belongs to a Run your access does not show in detail, so it was not read.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin for access to the Run's accounts.

<span id="RATE_LIMITED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>RATE_<wbr />LIMITED</code> · 429 · Retry: yes<br />A rate limit refused this request; details.limit names which (requests, quotes, prices, priceStreams, csvExports, exports, marketData and other camelCase names; an open set). The quotes limit admits one venue-hitting quote request every 3 seconds per caller; priceStreams counts open price streams.<br />**Do:** Wait for the Retry-After period (also details.retryAfter), then retry; for priceStreams, close a stream first.

<span id="REPLAY_WINDOW_FULL" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REPLAY_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />FULL</code> · 429 · Retry: yes<br />Reserved and not answered today: the shared nonce record has no per-key bound. If answered, nothing was done.<br />**Do:** Retry shortly with a fresh X-Timestamp and X-Nonce.

<span id="REQUEST_BODY_TOO_LARGE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REQUEST_<wbr />BODY_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />LARGE</code> · 413 · Retry: no<br />The request body is larger than allowed.<br />**Do:** Send a smaller body.

<span id="REQUEST_UNPROCESSABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REQUEST_<wbr />UNPROCESSABLE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The request body has the wrong shape for this operation.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="RESOURCE_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>RESOURCE_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The request conflicts with the resource's current state.<br />**Do:** Read the current resource before trying again.

<span id="RESOURCE_GONE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>RESOURCE_<wbr />GONE</code> · 410 · Retry: no<br />The resource is no longer available.<br />**Do:** Request a new resource.

<span id="RETURN_URL_NOT_REGISTERED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>RETURN_<wbr />URL_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />REGISTERED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />returnUrl is not an https URL whose exact origin (scheme, host and port) is one of the workspace's registered wallet callback origins, or it is on TetraFi's own app or signing origin.<br />**Do:** Register the origin under the workspace's embedded-wallet callbacks, or leave returnUrl out.

<span id="RETURN_URL_UNVERIFIABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>RETURN_<wbr />URL_<wbr />UNVERIFIABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's registered callback origins could not be read, so returnUrl could not be checked.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SCHEMA_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SCHEMA_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The order's signed payload does not match its schema.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SECOND_PROOF_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SECOND_<wbr />PROOF_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The step-up proof lacks the extra check the member set for orders, so the order was refused before any claim and nothing was placed (details.placementEffect noOrder).<br />**Do:** Complete the step-up again with the extra check, then send the order with the new proof.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The service account's own trading grant or signing key could not be read; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_CANNOT_REVIEW" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />CANNOT_<wbr />REVIEW</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />A service account cannot read or decide another principal's venue trade review; only human members review.<br />**Do:** Review the trade as a human member of the workspace.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The service account has expired.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin to renew it.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_INACTIVE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />INACTIVE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The service account is not active.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin to activate it.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The service account id is invalid.<br />**Do:** Check the key's service account.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The service account was not found in this workspace.<br />**Do:** Check the key's workspace.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_PLACE_ORDER_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />PLACE_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The service account's own role does not grant Place orders, so its venue trade was not placed.<br />**Do:** Give the service account a role with Place orders, then confirm a new quote.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_SPONSOR_MISSING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />SPONSOR_<wbr />MISSING</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The service account's sponsor is no longer a member of the workspace.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin to assign a new sponsor.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_SPONSOR_NOT_CURRENT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />SPONSOR_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CURRENT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The service account's sponsor, its accountable human, is no longer a current, unsuspended member of the workspace, so its key's organisation-wallet trades stop, including later steps of a trade already placed.<br />**Do:** Give the service account a current member as sponsor, or trade through another account.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />SUSPENDED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The service account is suspended.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin to lift the suspension.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_UNASSIGNED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />UNASSIGNED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The service account has no role.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin to assign a role.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_VENUE_KEY_NOT_CURRENT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />VENUE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CURRENT</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The service account, its sponsor or the signing key that prepared the venue trade is no longer current for venue trading; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Use the current service account's venue-trading key, with an active sponsor, for both the quote and the order.

<span id="SERVICE_ACCOUNT_WORKSPACE_MISSING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />MISSING</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The service account key has no workspace binding.<br />**Do:** Create a new key bound to the workspace.

<span id="SESSION_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SESSION_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The session's current authority could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SESSION_STORE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SESSION_<wbr />STORE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Session state is unavailable for a moment.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SIGNED_KEY_NOT_EXCHANGEABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNED_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />EXCHANGEABLE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The token endpoint (POST /api/v1/oauth/token) refused to exchange a key that requires request signing or holds venue trading: a bearer token would drop the per-request signature that key's authority rests on. It answers the RFC 6749 body, error unauthorized_client, with this code's reference in error_uri.<br />**Do:** Keep using the key directly and sign each request with its HMAC secret, or exchange a service-account key without request signing or venue trading.

<span id="STEP_UP_ALREADY_USED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>STEP_<wbr />UP_<wbr />ALREADY_<wbr />USED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This step-up proof was already used.<br />**Do:** Complete a new step-up and retry with its proof.

<span id="STEP_UP_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>STEP_<wbr />UP_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The step-up proof has expired.<br />**Do:** Complete a new step-up and retry with its proof.

<span id="STEP_UP_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>STEP_<wbr />UP_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This trade needs a fresh step-up confirmation; details.stepUpSubject names it.<br />**Do:** Complete the step-up for that subject and retry with stepUpProof.

<span id="STEP_UP_SUBJECT_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>STEP_<wbr />UP_<wbr />SUBJECT_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The step-up proof was made for a different action.<br />**Do:** Complete the step-up for the subject this refusal names.

<span id="STEP_UP_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>STEP_<wbr />UP_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The step-up check could not be performed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SUB_REQUEST_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SUB_<wbr />REQUEST_<wbr />BUDGET_<wbr />EXHAUSTED</code> · 429 · Retry: no<br />Answered as one item's status and body inside a 200 batch: the batch and its related reads made as many reads as one request may (200).<br />**Do:** Read the rest in another request.

<span id="UNSUPPORTED_CONTENT_TYPE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>UNSUPPORTED_<wbr />CONTENT_<wbr />TYPE</code> · 415 · Retry: no<br />The body is not sent as application/json.<br />**Do:** Send the body with Content-Type: application/json.

<span id="USER_NOT_IN_UMBRELLA" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>USER_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />IN_<wbr />UMBRELLA</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />The userId filter names a user outside the caller's umbrella.<br />**Do:** Filter by a user of a workspace under your umbrella.

<span id="WORKSPACE_ACCESS_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />ACCESS_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The credential's principal is no longer a current member of the workspace.<br />**Do:** Use a credential whose principal is a current workspace member.

<span id="WORKSPACE_CONTEXT_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />CONTEXT_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The credential is bound to another workspace than the one in the path.<br />**Do:** Call the workspace the credential belongs to.

<span id="WORKSPACE_CONTEXT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />CONTEXT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />A workspace context is required to act on this order.<br />**Do:** Send the request with the workspace's credential.

<span id="WORKSPACE_DAILY_TRADE_CAP_REACHED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />DAILY_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />CAP_<wbr />REACHED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This order would take the workspace's orders today (UTC) past the daily cap it set on its treasury settings (tradeCaps.dailyNotionalUsd, US dollars); nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount, wait for the next UTC day, or ask a workspace admin with Manage treasury policy to raise the cap in Treasury, Access &amp; policies, Approvals and limits.

<span id="WORKSPACE_HAS_DEFERRED_BALANCES" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />HAS_<wbr />DEFERRED_<wbr />BALANCES</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Members still hold deferred balances at this venue, so it is not disconnected; nothing changed.<br />**Do:** Settle the members' deferred balances at the venue, then disconnect it.

<span id="WORKSPACE_ID_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />ID_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The workspace id is not a valid workspace id.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="WORKSPACE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The credential's workspace id is invalid.<br />**Do:** Check the key's workspace.

<span id="WORKSPACE_NOT_ACTIVE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ACTIVE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The workspace is not active.<br />**Do:** Complete or restore the workspace, then retry.

<span id="WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />The path workspace is unknown or is not the authenticated workspace; both have the same response.<br />**Do:** Use your authenticated workspace id in the path.

<span id="WORKSPACE_PERMISSION_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />PERMISSION_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The credential's member lacks the workspace permission this read needs.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin for the permission; details.fix names who can grant it when a request can be made.

<span id="WORKSPACE_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />POLICY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's trading policy could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="WORKSPACE_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />A workspace is required for this request: the X-Workspace-Id header is missing or empty, or the action needs an authenticated workspace context.<br />**Do:** Send the request with a workspace-bound credential or workspace id.

<span id="WORKSPACE_SCOPE_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />SCOPE_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The header or other workspace scope selector is not available to this credential or session.<br />**Do:** Use a workspace the session belongs to and the credential is bound to.

<span id="WORKSPACE_TRADE_CAPS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />CAPS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's trade caps or its day total could not be read; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="WORKSPACE_TRADE_CAP_EXCEEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />CAP_<wbr />EXCEEDED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This order is above the per-trade cap the workspace set on its treasury settings (tradeCaps.perTradeUsd, US dollars); nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount, or ask a workspace admin with Manage treasury policy to raise the cap in Treasury, Access &amp; policies, Approvals and limits.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Quotes, prices and assets">

Asking venues for quotes, requoting, prices, assets, networks and routes. 83 codes.

<span id="AGGREGATION_TIMEOUT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>AGGREGATION_<wbr />TIMEOUT</code> · 504 · Retry: yes<br />No venue returned a valid quote before the deadline.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="AMOUNT_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>AMOUNT_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />An amount is not a positive exact decimal within the asset's precision.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="AMOUNT_PRECISION_EXCEEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>AMOUNT_<wbr />PRECISION_<wbr />EXCEEDED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The amount has more fractional digits than its asset allows; details names the field, decimals supplied and allowed precision.<br />**Do:** Use at most details.allowed fractional digits for details.field, then send the request again.

<span id="ASSETS_WORKSPACE_TYPE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ASSETS_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />TYPE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This workspace type cannot read taker assets.<br />**Do:** Use a Company, Broker or Direct-user workspace, or finish taker onboarding for this workspace.

<span id="ASSET_CATALOGUE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ASSET_<wbr />CATALOGUE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The asset catalogue is not loaded just now.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="ASSET_CATALOG_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ASSET_<wbr />CATALOG_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The asset catalogue could not be read to check the deposit network.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="ASSET_FILTER_BLANK" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ASSET_<wbr />FILTER_<wbr />BLANK</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />An asset-list filter was sent with an empty value, which is never read as absent; details.field names it.<br />**Do:** Give the filter a value, or leave it out.

<span id="ASSET_FILTER_INCOMPLETE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ASSET_<wbr />FILTER_<wbr />INCOMPLETE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />An asset-list filter needs another one beside it (venueCode needs venueId, address needs network); details.field names it.<br />**Do:** Add the filter it needs, or leave it out.

<span id="ASSET_HAS_NO_CHAIN_DEPOSIT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ASSET_<wbr />HAS_<wbr />NO_<wbr />CHAIN_<wbr />DEPOSIT</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The selected asset has no on-chain deposit.<br />**Do:** Read its bank-deposit instructions by sending the asset without a network, or select an asset with an on-chain token.

<span id="ASSET_ISSUER_EXCLUDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ASSET_<wbr />ISSUER_<wbr />EXCLUDED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />An Asset in the request is issued by an asset issuer the workspace excluded in its counterparty management; details.issuerId names the issuer and details.asset the Asset.<br />**Do:** Include the issuer again in counterparty management, or quote another Asset.

<span id="ASSET_ISSUER_UNVERIFIABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ASSET_<wbr />ISSUER_<wbr />UNVERIFIABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />An Asset of the held quote is no longer in the Asset catalogue, so whether its issuer is excluded could not be checked; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="ASSET_NOT_ON_NETWORK" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ASSET_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ON_<wbr />NETWORK</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The asset has no deployment on the network pinned for that side.<br />**Do:** Pin a network the asset is on (see GET /assets), or remove the pin.

<span id="ASSET_UNKNOWN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ASSET_<wbr />UNKNOWN</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The asset is not in the catalogue; details.value names it.<br />**Do:** Use an asset listed by GET /assets.

<span id="BRIDGE_FEES_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BRIDGE_<wbr />FEES_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The fees of the bridge details.provider names could not be read; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="BRIDGE_FEE_SHARE_TOO_HIGH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BRIDGE_<wbr />FEE_<wbr />SHARE_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />HIGH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The bridge fee exceeds details.limitPercent percent of the amount at the bridge details.provider names; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Increase the amount or choose another quote.

<span id="BUDGET_EXHAUSTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BUDGET_<wbr />EXHAUSTED</code> · 429 · Retry: yes<br />This venue's quote budget is used up for now.<br />**Do:** Wait for the Retry-After period, then requote.

<span id="CONVERSION_IDENTITY_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONVERSION_<wbr />IDENTITY_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue trade already belongs to another operation; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Read the existing operation, or request a new quote for a new trade.

<span id="CONVERSION_IDENTITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONVERSION_<wbr />IDENTITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The trade's identity could not be recorded; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="CONVERSION_PAIR_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONVERSION_<wbr />PAIR_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The trade's pair does not match the approved conversion; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="CORRIDOR_NOT_OFFERED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CORRIDOR_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />OFFERED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This quote was not offered on the corridor the request names.<br />**Do:** Requote on a corridor the quote lists, or request new quotes.

<span id="CORRIDOR_NOT_SUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CORRIDOR_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />SUPPORTED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />No settlement path is live on both ends of this network pair.<br />**Do:** Choose a different network pair or quote.

<span id="COUNTERPARTY_NOT_READY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COUNTERPARTY_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />READY</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as the hint of a 200 pair or asset list, never as an HTTP status: the workspace's connected venues list nothing yet, because each is still being set up, has trading paused, or needs Advanced while it is not available.<br />**Do:** Finish setting up or resume a venue in Venues (GET /api/v1/pairs?availability=connectable names each with its state), or connect another.

<span id="DELIVER_TO_AMBIGUOUS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELIVER_<wbr />TO_<wbr />AMBIGUOUS</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The deliverTo address matches more than one withdrawal destination. A quote source answers unavailable with it; an order or requote on that source is refused.<br />**Do:** Name the destination exactly, including its tag or memo.

<span id="DELIVER_TO_NETWORK_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELIVER_<wbr />TO_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The delivery address is not on the trade's receive network.<br />**Do:** Set deliverTo.network to the receive network named in details.receiveNetwork, or choose a different receive network.

<span id="DELIVER_TO_NOT_A_WORKSPACE_WALLET" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELIVER_<wbr />TO_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />A_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />WALLET</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />deliverTo is not one of the workspace's own wallets. A quote source answers unavailable with it; an order or requote on that source is refused.<br />**Do:** Deliver to a workspace wallet.

<span id="DELIVER_TO_NOT_REGISTERED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELIVER_<wbr />TO_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />REGISTERED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The deliverTo address is not a wallet or saved destination of this workspace that the caller may deliver to. No venue was asked.<br />**Do:** Leave deliverTo out to receive in the paying wallet, or name a registered wallet or destination.

<span id="DELIVER_TO_NOT_SIGNING_WALLET" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELIVER_<wbr />TO_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />SIGNING_<wbr />WALLET</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />A client-signed trade delivers to the wallet that signs it, and deliverTo names another destination (details.field is deliverTo). No venue was asked.<br />**Do:** Leave deliverTo out to receive in the signing wallet, or name that wallet.

<span id="DESK_FIRM_RESERVED_FOR_EXECUTORS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DESK_<wbr />FIRM_<wbr />RESERVED_<wbr />FOR_<wbr />EXECUTORS</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />Firm prices from this venue are kept for members who can place orders: today's remaining firm asks are the share reserved for them.<br />**Do:** Use the venue's indicative price, ask a member with Place orders, or retry after 00:00 UTC.

<span id="EXACT_ROUTE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>EXACT_<wbr />ROUTE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />No executable route exists for this exact pair right now.<br />**Do:** Change the pair or amount, or try again later.

<span id="MORE_COUNTERPARTIES_AVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>MORE_<wbr />COUNTERPARTIES_<wbr />AVAILABLE</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as the hint of a 200 asset list or quote set, never as an HTTP status: the workspace has venues enabled, and connecting more can add assets and prices.<br />**Do:** Connect more counterparties in Venues.

<span id="NETWORK_PIN_NEEDS_ONCHAIN_ASSET" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>NETWORK_<wbr />PIN_<wbr />NEEDS_<wbr />ONCHAIN_<wbr />ASSET</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />A network was pinned on a side that is not an on-chain asset.<br />**Do:** Remove the network pin, or name an on-chain asset.

<span id="NETWORK_REGISTRY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>NETWORK_<wbr />REGISTRY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The network list could not be read, so the networks could not be checked.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="NETWORK_UNKNOWN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>NETWORK_<wbr />UNKNOWN</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The network is not known; details.value names it.<br />**Do:** Use a network listed by GET /assets.

<span id="NO_COUNTERPARTY_CONNECTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>NO_<wbr />COUNTERPARTY_<wbr />CONNECTED</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as the hint of a 200 asset list or quote set, never as an HTTP status: no venue the workspace has enabled lists anything, so the list is empty.<br />**Do:** Connect a counterparty in Venues (a venue key, or enable a key-less on-chain venue), then read again.

<span id="NO_ELIGIBLE_VENUE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>NO_<wbr />ELIGIBLE_<wbr />VENUE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />No venue is eligible for this request; details.reason says why (noEligibleVenue; venuesUnavailable when the venues that could serve it named their refusal, which the message lists; compositeLegFailed with details.routeFailures). details.venuesNotEnabledForWorkspace names venues this workspace has not enabled.<br />**Do:** Change the pair, amount or filters, or connect a venue that serves it; for venuesUnavailable, request quotes again once the venue is ready.

<span id="NO_QUOTE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>NO_<wbr />QUOTE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />Nothing quoted the request; details.reason says why (allVenuesFailed: every venue asked refused or failed, the message lists their answers; insufficientLiquidity: no venue has enough liquidity for this amount; requestNotPriceable: the request cannot be priced as asked).<br />**Do:** Change the pair, amount or filters, then request quotes again; for insufficientLiquidity try a smaller amount or later.

<span id="PRICES_ELIGIBILITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PRICES_<wbr />ELIGIBILITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Which venues may price for this caller could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="PRICES_ELIGIBILITY_WARMING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PRICES_<wbr />ELIGIBILITY_<wbr />WARMING</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Price eligibility is still loading.<br />**Do:** Retry after the Retry-After period (about one second).

<span id="PRICES_IDENTITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PRICES_<wbr />IDENTITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The current market identities could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="PRICES_POLICIES_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PRICES_<wbr />POLICIES_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's pricing policies could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="PRICES_SNAPSHOT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PRICES_<wbr />SNAPSHOT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · Stream event · Retry: yes<br />Sent on the price stream when the current price inputs could not be verified; the stream ends.<br />**Do:** Open a new stream subscription shortly.

<span id="PRICES_WORKSPACE_TYPE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PRICES_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />TYPE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This workspace type cannot read prices.<br />**Do:** Use a Company, Broker or Direct-user workspace, or finish taker onboarding.

<span id="PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PRODUCT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The venue is not offering this product right now.<br />**Do:** Choose another pair or venue.

<span id="QUOTE_ADMISSION_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />ADMISSION_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The quote's admission state could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="QUOTE_ALREADY_ORDERED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />ALREADY_<wbr />ORDERED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This quote was already used for an order; details.orderId names that order when the refusing step knows it.<br />**Do:** Read the existing order (GET /orders/&#123;orderId&#125;, or GET /orders/by-quote/&#123;quoteId&#125;); do not place it again.

<span id="QUOTE_BELOW_EXECUTION_COST" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />BELOW_<wbr />EXECUTION_<wbr />COST</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The amount does not cover the estimated execution cost.<br />**Do:** Use a larger amount.

<span id="QUOTE_BOUND_BREACH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />BOUND_<wbr />BREACH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The quote is outside the workspace's approved price bounds; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="QUOTE_BOUND_EVIDENCE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />BOUND_<wbr />EVIDENCE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The evidence for the workspace's price bounds could not be read; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="QUOTE_CANNOT_BE_REFRESHED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />CANNOT_<wbr />BE_<wbr />REFRESHED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The quote was recorded before it could be refreshed for a client.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again.

<span id="QUOTE_CATALOGUE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />CATALOGUE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The asset catalogue is not loaded just now.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="QUOTE_CLAIM_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />CLAIM_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The quote's claim could not be recorded.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="QUOTE_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 410 · Retry: no<br />The quote has expired.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="QUOTE_ID_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />ID_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The quote id cannot be used; details.reason is malformed and details.remediation correctField.<br />**Do:** Send the quoteId exactly as POST /quotes returned it.

<span id="QUOTE_ID_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />ID_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />A quote request id could not be issued.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="QUOTE_INTEGRITY_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />INTEGRITY_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The order needs the original authenticated quote.<br />**Do:** Send the quote exactly as returned.

<span id="QUOTE_LIFE_BELOW_SIGNING_BUDGET" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />LIFE_<wbr />BELOW_<wbr />SIGNING_<wbr />BUDGET</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The quote has too little life left for signing; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="QUOTE_NETWORK_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The quote payload is missing its execution network.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="QUOTE_NOT_EXECUTABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />EXECUTABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This price is for comparison only.<br />**Do:** Request an executable quote first.

<span id="QUOTE_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />No quote with this id is available to the caller; details.reason is unknown and details.remediation requestNewQuote.<br />**Do:** Check the quoteId, or request a new quote.

<span id="QUOTE_NOT_HELD" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />HELD</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The quote is no longer held; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="QUOTE_NOT_READY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />READY</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The quote is still being recorded.<br />**Do:** Retry after about one second.

<span id="QUOTE_ORDER_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The quote's order could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the read shortly; do not place another order until it succeeds.

<span id="QUOTE_OUTSIDE_APPROVED_BOUNDS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />OUTSIDE_<wbr />APPROVED_<wbr />BOUNDS</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The quote is outside the approved bounds; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="QUOTE_PRICE_MOVED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />PRICE_<wbr />MOVED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The price moved outside the order's protection.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, or review the repricing offer if the response provides one.

<span id="QUOTE_RECONFIRMATION_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />RECONFIRMATION_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue's firm price could no longer be accepted when this order reached it; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="QUOTE_REQUEST_KIND_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />REQUEST_<wbr />KIND_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The quote request kind is not pair or requote.<br />**Do:** Use kind: pair or kind: requote with that kind's documented fields.

<span id="QUOTE_REQUEST_KIND_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />REQUEST_<wbr />KIND_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The quote request is missing its required kind tag.<br />**Do:** Send kind: pair for a fresh quote or kind: requote to refresh one.

<span id="QUOTE_REQUEST_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />REQUEST_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />No quote request with this id is visible to the caller.<br />**Do:** Check the quoteRequestId.

<span id="QUOTE_REQUEST_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />REQUEST_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The quote request could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="QUOTE_STORE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />STORE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Held quotes cannot be read right now.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="QUOTE_VALID_UNTIL_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />VALID_<wbr />UNTIL_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The quote carries no validity deadline, so it cannot be submitted.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="QUOTE_WALLET_NOT_BOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>QUOTE_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />BOUND</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The quote was priced before a paying wallet was chosen (its tradingSource is null and its next is requote); nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Choose the wallet to pay from, requote, then order the new quote.

<span id="REFERENCE_PRICE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REFERENCE_<wbr />PRICE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />A reference price for the bounds check could not be read; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote shortly.

<span id="REQUOTE_FIELDS_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REQUOTE_<wbr />FIELDS_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />A requote body carries fields that cannot go with requote; details.conflicts lists them.<br />**Do:** Send requote alone, or send a fresh quote request.

<span id="REQUOTE_NEEDS_WALLET" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REQUOTE_<wbr />NEEDS_<wbr />WALLET</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue quotes once a wallet is chosen.<br />**Do:** Bind a wallet, then requote.

<span id="REQUOTE_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REQUOTE_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue would not requote; details.reason says why.<br />**Do:** Act on details.reason, or request fresh quotes.

<span id="REQUOTE_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REQUOTE_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The firm order's handoff could not be read, so it needs a new quote.<br />**Do:** Requote, then sign the replacement.

<span id="REQUOTE_TIMED_OUT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REQUOTE_<wbr />TIMED_<wbr />OUT</code> · 504 · Retry: yes<br />The venue did not answer the requote in time; the original quote still holds.<br />**Do:** Requote again, or use the original quote before it expires.

<span id="REQUOTE_WINDOW_CLOSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REQUOTE_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />CLOSED</code> · 410 · Retry: no<br />The quote is past its requote window (retainUntil), so it can no longer be refreshed or ordered.<br />**Do:** Search again: POST /quotes with a pair body.

<span id="ROUTING_OPTIONS_FORBIDDEN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ROUTING_<wbr />OPTIONS_<wbr />FORBIDDEN</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The request used an option reserved for TetraFi operators.<br />**Do:** Remove the reserved option and retry.

<span id="SAME_ASSET_NEEDS_NETWORK" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SAME_<wbr />ASSET_<wbr />NEEDS_<wbr />NETWORK</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />Paying and receiving the same asset needs a network pinned on each side.<br />**Do:** Pin the pay and receive networks.

<span id="USABLE_SET_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>USABLE_<wbr />SET_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The wallets this caller may use could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_QUOTE_RATE_LIMITED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />RATE_<wbr />LIMITED</code> · 429 · Retry: yes<br />The named venue is limiting quote requests; the request remains counted and nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Wait for Retry-After (also details.retryAfter), then deliberately request again; details.reason names the retry timing source.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Orders">

Placing, following and cancelling an order: minimum receive, slippage, caps, the source it trades from and its execution. 95 codes.

<span id="ADVANCED_ACCOUNT_ELIGIBILITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ADVANCED_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />ELIGIBILITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Whether the route's venue account allows Advanced trading could not be read; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.
