<span id="ADVANCED_MODE_DISABLED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ADVANCED_<wbr />MODE_<wbr />DISABLED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This platform has not enabled Advanced (TetraFi's router, escrow and settlement contracts, and the end-user personal wallets only Advanced serves); nothing was placed or created.<br />**Do:** Use a Basic quote or an organisation wallet, or wait until Advanced is enabled.

<span id="ADVANCED_SOURCE_ACCOUNT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ADVANCED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The selected trading source's accounts could not be read, or the selected financial account does not resolve to one wallet; no venue was asked.<br />**Do:** Retry shortly; if it persists, choose another trading source.

<span id="ADVANCED_VENUE_ACCOUNT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ADVANCED_<wbr />VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This route needs an Advanced venue account that the workspace does not have; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Set up the venue account for Advanced trading, then request a new quote.

<span id="CLIENT_ORDER_ID_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CLIENT_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />ID_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />clientOrderId is not 1-64 ASCII letters, digits, underscores or hyphens.<br />**Do:** Correct clientOrderId and send the request again.

<span id="CLIENT_ORDER_ID_IN_USE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CLIENT_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />ID_<wbr />IN_<wbr />USE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The workspace already has a non-terminal order or in-flight placement with this clientOrderId.<br />**Do:** Find the order by clientOrderId, or retry its original Idempotency-Key.

<span id="DIRECT_DEX_EVIDENCE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DIRECT_<wbr />DEX_<wbr />EVIDENCE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The swap route's preflight record could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="DIRECT_DEX_PREFLIGHT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DIRECT_<wbr />DEX_<wbr />PREFLIGHT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This swap route needs a matching preflight before the order.<br />**Do:** Call POST /orders/preflight for this quote, then place the order.

<span id="DIRECT_DEX_ROUTE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DIRECT_<wbr />DEX_<wbr />ROUTE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The swap route does not match the expected shape.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="DIRECT_USER_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DIRECT_<wbr />USER_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The order names a different direct user than its quote.<br />**Do:** Send the onBehalfOf the quote was made for.

<span id="EXECUTION_NOTIONAL_UNVALUED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>EXECUTION_<wbr />NOTIONAL_<wbr />UNVALUED</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The trade's value could not be checked against the venue's limits; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="EXECUTION_STAGE_DISABLED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>EXECUTION_<wbr />STAGE_<wbr />DISABLED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Trading on this venue is switched off in this environment; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Choose another venue's quote.

<span id="EXECUTION_STOPPED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>EXECUTION_<wbr />STOPPED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Execution is stopped for this venue or route; nothing was placed or sent.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote; retry later if the venue resumes.

<span id="EXECUTION_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>EXECUTION_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The route's execution path is not available, so no plan could be sealed.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote.

<span id="FIRM_ORDER_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIRM_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The firm order must exist before its signature is submitted.<br />**Do:** Place the order first, then submit the signature.

<span id="FIRM_QUOTE_IN_FLIGHT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIRM_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />IN_<wbr />FLIGHT</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />A firm price for this quote is already being requested.<br />**Do:** Wait a moment and read the order; do not send a second order on the same quote.

<span id="FIRM_QUOTE_REQUEST_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIRM_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />REQUEST_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This quote was already firmed for a different order (amount, wallet or pair).<br />**Do:** Request a new quote for this order.

<span id="HELD_QUOTE_SOURCE_UNRESOLVED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>HELD_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />UNRESOLVED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The held quote's trading source or exact venue account can no longer be resolved; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="HIGH_SLIPPAGE_ACK_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>HIGH_<wbr />SLIPPAGE_<wbr />ACK_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The quote's slippage is above the threshold that needs acknowledgement.<br />**Do:** Acknowledge the high slippage in the request, or choose a better quote.

<span id="INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INSUFFICIENT_<wbr />BALANCE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The paying wallet does not hold enough of the input asset.<br />**Do:** Add funds or lower the amount, then place the order again.

<span id="INSUFFICIENT_LP_BALANCE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INSUFFICIENT_<wbr />LP_<wbr />BALANCE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The workspace's balance with this liquidity provider does not cover the order.<br />**Do:** Deposit funds with the provider, then place the order again.

<span id="LEGACY_CEX_LANE_RETIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LEGACY_<wbr />CEX_<wbr />LANE_<wbr />RETIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This liquidity provider's old connection path is retired.<br />**Do:** Connect the provider as a venue account, then request new quotes.

<span id="MIN_RECEIVE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>MIN_<wbr />RECEIVE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The minimum receive is not a positive amount.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="MIN_RECEIVE_NOT_ACCEPTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>MIN_<wbr />RECEIVE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ACCEPTED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This quote executes at the price shown, so it takes no minimum receive.<br />**Do:** Remove minReceive from the order.

<span id="MIN_RECEIVE_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>MIN_<wbr />RECEIVE_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This venue needs the least you accept to receive.<br />**Do:** Set minReceive on the order.

<span id="OPERATION_NOT_SUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>OPERATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />SUPPORTED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This order does not support that operation.<br />**Do:** Use an operation the order's type supports.

<span id="ORDER_ADMISSION_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />ADMISSION_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Whether this order is already registered could not be confirmed.<br />**Do:** Retry with the same request shortly; it will not create a second order.

<span id="ORDER_CANCELLED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />CANCELLED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This order was cancelled before dispatch; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Read the order; request a new quote to trade again.

<span id="ORDER_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The order's signed payload has expired.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="ORDER_HELD_FOR_REVIEW" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />HELD_<wbr />FOR_<wbr />REVIEW</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The order is held for compliance review; a source transaction may already be confirmed.<br />**Do:** Read the order and wait for the review outcome; do not place it or send its source again.

<span id="ORDER_ID_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />ID_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This order id is already registered for a different request.<br />**Do:** Read the existing order, or request a new quote.

<span id="ORDER_IN_PROGRESS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />IN_<wbr />PROGRESS</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />This order is still being placed.<br />**Do:** Wait for the Retry-After period, then read the order.

<span id="ORDER_NOT_CANCELLABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CANCELLABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This order already dispatched or its funding or delivery progressed.<br />**Do:** Read the current order status; cancellation changed nothing.

<span id="ORDER_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />No order with this id is visible to the caller.<br />**Do:** Check the order id and the workspace.

<span id="ORDER_PLACEMENT_CAPACITY_BUSY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />PLACEMENT_<wbr />CAPACITY_<wbr />BUSY</code> · 429 · Retry: yes<br />Every order placement slot was busy past this request's wait, so the order was refused before anything was held or sent.<br />**Do:** Wait for the Retry-After period, then send the same request again.

<span id="ORDER_PROVENANCE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />PROVENANCE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The trade's workspace ownership could not be read, so the trade was not shown.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="ORDER_RECORD_PENDING_RECOVERY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />RECORD_<wbr />PENDING_<wbr />RECOVERY</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />The source transaction ran, but the order could not be recorded yet; the quote claim is kept.<br />**Do:** Register the same source transaction again to recover the order; do not sign or send another trade.

<span id="ORDER_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This order was refused by a pre-trade check before anything was sent; a refused order is final.<br />**Do:** Read the order for the reason, clear it, then request a new quote and place a new order.

<span id="ORDER_SIGNING_AUTHORIZATION_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />SIGNING_<wbr />AUTHORIZATION_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />A retained authorization cannot be combined with a new owner proof.<br />**Do:** Send either the retained authorization or a new proof, not both.

<span id="ORDER_SIGNING_CONTEXT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />SIGNING_<wbr />CONTEXT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The order's signing needs a workspace and signer context.<br />**Do:** Send the signer context the preparation asked for.

<span id="ORDER_SIGNING_SOURCE_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />SIGNING_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The order names conflicting signing sources.<br />**Do:** Name one signing source.

<span id="ORDER_SIGNING_WALLET_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />SIGNING_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The signing wallet is not the wallet the order was prepared for.<br />**Do:** Sign with the prepared wallet, or prepare the order again.

<span id="ORDER_SIZE_EXCEEDS_LP_LIMIT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />SIZE_<wbr />EXCEEDS_<wbr />LP_<wbr />LIMIT</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The amount is above the liquidity provider's limit.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount.

<span id="ORDER_TYPE_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ORDER_<wbr />TYPE_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This order type cannot be signed this way.<br />**Do:** Use a quote whose order type supports the chosen signing.

<span id="PERIOD_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PERIOD_<wbr />CAPACITY_<wbr />EXCEEDED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The trade would exceed the workspace's approved capacity for this period; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount, or wait for the next period.

<span id="PREFLIGHT_NOT_APPLICABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PREFLIGHT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />APPLICABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This quote trades on a venue account and needs no preflight.<br />**Do:** Place the order directly.

<span id="PRICE_ACCOUNT_NOT_PRICED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PRICE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />PRICED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue account the priceId was read on has no orderable price for its size now (its connection is gone, or the venue priced only another of the workspace's accounts); nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Read Live prices again and order the fresh priceId.

<span id="PRICE_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PRICE_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The priceId's validFor lapsed before the order arrived; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Read Live prices again and order the fresh priceId at once.

<span id="PRICE_ID_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PRICE_<wbr />ID_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The priceId was not issued to this workspace by this server, or was altered; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Read Live prices again and order a tradable row's priceId as returned.

<span id="PRICE_ID_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PRICE_<wbr />ID_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The priceId could not be checked right now; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="PRICE_PROTECTION_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PRICE_<wbr />PROTECTION_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Changing price protection needs a new price; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request the price again with the new protection.

<span id="RECORDED_SLIPPAGE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>RECORDED_<wbr />SLIPPAGE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The historical order has a slippage tolerance that cannot be represented as whole basis points.<br />**Do:** Request reconciliation of the historical record; do not resubmit the order.

<span id="RECOVERY_RECEIVER_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>RECOVERY_<wbr />RECEIVER_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The recovery receiver must be the wallet that signed the funding.<br />**Do:** Use the funding signer as the receiver.

<span id="REFIRM_ALREADY_BROADCAST" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REFIRM_<wbr />ALREADY_<wbr />BROADCAST</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />A fill was already broadcast for this order, so its price cannot be firmed again.<br />**Do:** Read the order and follow its fill.

<span id="REFIRM_CAP_EXCEEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REFIRM_<wbr />CAP_<wbr />EXCEEDED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The order was re-firmed as many times as the venue allows.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote for a new order.

<span id="REFIRM_NOT_YET" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REFIRM_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />YET</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The order's current firm quote is still live, so it cannot be re-firmed yet.<br />**Do:** Retry after the current firm quote has lapsed.

<span id="REFIRM_ORDER_CLOSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REFIRM_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />CLOSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The order is final, or is a route order, so its price cannot be firmed again.<br />**Do:** Read the order; request a new quote if you still want to trade.

<span id="REFIRM_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REFIRM_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue declined to firm this order's price again.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote for a new order.

<span id="REFIRM_REPLAYED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REFIRM_<wbr />REPLAYED</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The venue answered with a firm price this order already used, so no new firm price was taken.<br />**Do:** Retry the continuation, or request a new quote.

<span id="REFIRM_SOURCE_STATE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REFIRM_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />STATE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The order's source record could not be read, so whether a fill was sent is unknown; nothing was firmed again.<br />**Do:** Read the order, then retry shortly if it is still waiting.

<span id="REFIRM_SUPERSEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REFIRM_<wbr />SUPERSEDED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Another request firmed this order again first; that firm price is the current one.<br />**Do:** Read the order and continue with its current firm price.

<span id="REFIRM_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REFIRM_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This venue does not firm an existing order's price again.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote for a new order.

<span id="REPLACEMENT_FEE_ABOVE_APPROVED_CAP" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REPLACEMENT_<wbr />FEE_<wbr />ABOVE_<wbr />APPROVED_<wbr />CAP</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as a 200 step transaction's replacementFailureCode, never as an HTTP status: replacing the stuck send would cost more network fee than its approved cap, so it was not replaced.<br />**Do:** A person re-sends the stuck transaction, or approves a higher fee cap.

<span id="REPLACEMENT_FEE_CAP_NOT_APPROVED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REPLACEMENT_<wbr />FEE_<wbr />CAP_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />APPROVED</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as a 200 step transaction's replacementFailureCode, never as an HTTP status: the stuck send has no approved fee cap for a replacement, so it was not replaced.<br />**Do:** A person re-sends the stuck transaction, or approves a fee cap for its replacement.

<span id="REPRICING_OFFER_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REPRICING_<wbr />OFFER_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />There is no repricing offer to accept.<br />**Do:** Prepare the continuation again without acceptRepricing.

<span id="ROUTE_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ROUTE_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The route's execution authority could not be confirmed.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote.

<span id="ROUTE_NOT_WAITING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ROUTE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />WAITING</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The order is not waiting for the taker: it is settled, still running a step, or has no continuation, so there is nothing to prepare, sign or stop.<br />**Do:** Read the order; act only when its nextAction asks for the taker.

<span id="ROUTE_SOURCE_EVIDENCE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ROUTE_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />EVIDENCE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The source transaction hash is invalid.<br />**Do:** Send the source transaction hash exactly.

<span id="ROUTE_SOURCE_PLAN_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ROUTE_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />PLAN_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The original source plan could not be verified; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="ROUTE_SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ROUTE_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The route's source setup is not available.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote.

<span id="ROUTE_TRADE_INTENT_NOT_APPLICABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ROUTE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />INTENT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />APPLICABLE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This continuation takes funderSignature and funderPermit2, not a route trade intent.<br />**Do:** Send funderSignature and funderPermit2 instead.

<span id="ROUTE_TRADE_INTENT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ROUTE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />INTENT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This continuation needs routeTradeIntent and routeTradeIntentSignature.<br />**Do:** Include both fields.

<span id="ROUTE_TRADE_INTENT_TOO_LARGE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ROUTE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />INTENT_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />LARGE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />routeTradeIntent is larger than allowed.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="SELF_DEALING_CHECK_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SELF_<wbr />DEALING_<wbr />CHECK_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Whether the quote's provider is the workspace's own liquidity could not be read, so the self-dealing check could not run; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SELF_DEALING_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SELF_<wbr />DEALING_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The quote comes from the workspace's own liquidity (a solver of its own LP); a workspace cannot trade with itself, so nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Choose a quote from another provider.

<span id="SOURCE_EXECUTION_POLICY_REJECTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOURCE_<wbr />EXECUTION_<wbr />POLICY_<wbr />REJECTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The workspace router's compliance policy refused the source transaction.<br />**Do:** Do not re-sign; the policy verdict must change first. Contact TetraFi support with the requestId if this is unexpected.

<span id="SOURCE_LEG_RECOVERED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOURCE_<wbr />LEG_<wbr />RECOVERED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The source transaction succeeded but its funds were returned to the payer instead of starting the route.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote for a new order.

<span id="SOURCE_NOT_OWNED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOURCE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />OWNED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />A source names a wallet or account this caller cannot use: unknown, outside this workspace, or outside the key's grants.<br />**Do:** Name a wallet or account from the answer's sourceOptions.

<span id="SOURCE_RECOVERY_CLAIM_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOURCE_<wbr />RECOVERY_<wbr />CLAIM_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This source does not belong to the retained quote claim.<br />**Do:** Register the source of this order's own claim.

<span id="SOURCE_RECOVERY_QUOTE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOURCE_<wbr />RECOVERY_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The recovery quote does not match its held terms.<br />**Do:** Use the recovery quote exactly as held.

<span id="SOURCE_RECOVERY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOURCE_<wbr />RECOVERY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The retained recovery claim could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry shortly; do not send another trade.

<span id="SOURCE_TIMEOUT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOURCE_<wbr />TIMEOUT</code> · 504 · Retry: yes<br />Answered as one item's status and body inside a 200 batch: that item had not answered when the request's own deadline came, so the other items kept their answers. A list that composes several sources names a source that ran past its own deadline as an unreachable entry with reason timeout instead, beside the rows the other sources returned.<br />**Do:** Read that item again, on its own if it keeps timing out.

<span id="SOURCE_WORKSPACE_ID_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOURCE_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />ID_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The sourceWorkspaceId filter is not a workspace id.<br />**Do:** Send a workspace UUID, or leave the filter out.

<span id="SUBMIT_SLIPPAGE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SUBMIT_<wbr />SLIPPAGE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The submitted slippage tolerance is not a non-negative integer in basis points.<br />**Do:** Use whole basis points in deferredOptions.submitSlippageBps.

<span id="SWAP_SLIPPAGE_TOLERANCE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SWAP_<wbr />SLIPPAGE_<wbr />TOLERANCE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The workspace's swap slippage tolerance could not be read, so no minimum could be set for the swap; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Set the workspace's swapSlippageToleranceBps again, then request a new quote.

<span id="TRADING_SOURCE_NOT_LINKED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TRADING_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />LINKED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The selected trading source is not linked to the venue account this quote would use.<br />**Do:** Choose a linked trading source, or link it on the venue account, then request quotes again.

<span id="TRANSPORT_PATHWAY_UNREACHABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TRANSPORT_<wbr />PATHWAY_<wbr />UNREACHABLE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The settlement transport for this network pair has no live path.<br />**Do:** Choose a different network pair or quote.

<span id="VENUE_QUOTE_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue's firm price expired before the order could use it.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="VENUE_QUOTE_NOT_PRICED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />PRICED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue returned no price for the firm ask; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request the price again.

<span id="VENUE_QUOTE_OPERATION_BOUNDS_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />OPERATION_<wbr />BOUNDS_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The firm price does not match the order's bounds; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="VENUE_QUOTE_OPERATION_CLOSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />OPERATION_<wbr />CLOSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The firm-price preparation is expired or already dispatched; this code does not establish whether a trade was sent.<br />**Do:** Read the preparation and its existing dispatch before requesting another quote.

<span id="VENUE_QUOTE_OUTSIDE_BOUNDS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />OUTSIDE_<wbr />BOUNDS</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue's firm price is below the order's minimum receive; nothing was sent. details.remediation names the remedy: widenLimit, with details.currentReceive (what the venue's price delivers) and details.requiredSlippageBps (the tolerance below the quote that price needs), or requote. Treat an unknown remediation as contactSupport.<br />**Do:** Act on details.remediation: widenLimit, place again with a minimum receive at or below details.currentReceive; requote, request a new price.

<span id="VENUE_QUOTE_SEND_WINDOW_CLOSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />SEND_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />CLOSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The order arrived before the quote's expiresAt, but the venue's firm price reached its send deadline before it could be sent; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="WORKSPACE_ORDER_PLACING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />PLACING</code> · 429 · Retry: yes<br />Another order in this workspace was still being placed past this request's wait, so this order was refused before anything was held or sent.<br />**Do:** Wait for the Retry-After period, then send the same request again.

<span id="WORKSPACE_SLIPPAGE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />SLIPPAGE_<wbr />LIMIT_<wbr />EXCEEDED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The quote's slippage is above the workspace's limit.<br />**Do:** Choose a quote within the limit, or ask an admin to change it.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Signing and grants">

Signing grants, wallet signatures, signed steps and owner authorizations. 47 codes.

<span id="EIP712_HASH_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>EIP712_<wbr />HASH_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 500 · Retry: no<br />TetraFi could not hash the order's typed-data payload; nothing was signed or sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="EXPIRED_BEFORE_SIGNATURE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>EXPIRED_<wbr />BEFORE_<wbr />SIGNATURE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The quote expired before signing; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="FUNDER_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDER_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The funder is not a 20-byte 0x-hex address.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="FUNDER_PERMIT_NOT_APPLICABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDER_<wbr />PERMIT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />APPLICABLE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This continuation is signed as a route trade intent, not a funder permit.<br />**Do:** Send routeTradeIntent and routeTradeIntentSignature instead.

<span id="FUNDER_PERMIT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDER_<wbr />PERMIT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This continuation needs funderSignature and funderPermit2.<br />**Do:** Include funderSignature and funderPermit2.

<span id="GRANT_ENDS_BEFORE_VENUE_FUNDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>GRANT_<wbr />ENDS_<wbr />BEFORE_<wbr />VENUE_<wbr />FUNDING</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet's signing grant expires before the venue's funding window; no trade acceptance or funds were sent.<br />**Do:** Renew the wallet's StableFX signing grant for at least 2 days, longer than the venue's 24-hour funding window plus its safety margin.

<span id="NATIVE_BALANCE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>NATIVE_<wbr />BALANCE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · Stream event · Retry: yes<br />Sent as the reason of a 200 read's unavailable wallet balance, never as an HTTP status: the network's native-coin balance read failed, so no balance is shown.<br />**Do:** Read the balance again shortly.

<span id="NATIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY_REAPPROVAL_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>NATIVE_<wbr />GRANT_<wbr />EXPIRY_<wbr />REAPPROVAL_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet's installed trading grant needs owner reapproval; no preparation or funds were sent.<br />**Do:** Have the wallet's owners reapprove its trading grant, then request a new quote.

<span id="OWNER_AUTHORIZATION_NOT_ACCEPTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>OWNER_<wbr />AUTHORIZATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ACCEPTED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This quote is paid from a wallet that signs itself, so it takes a signature, not an owner approval.<br />**Do:** Send the wallet's signature instead.

<span id="PERMIT_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PERMIT_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />funderPermit2 does not match the prepared permit.<br />**Do:** Sign the permit exactly as prepared.

<span id="RELEASE_GRANT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>RELEASE_<wbr />GRANT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The approval to release this trade could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="RELEASE_WINDOW_LAPSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>RELEASE_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />LAPSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The approval window for releasing this trade has passed; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SIGNATURE_FORMAT_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNATURE_<wbr />FORMAT_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The signature is not 65 bytes.<br />**Do:** Send the 65-byte signature as hex.

<span id="SIGNATURE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNATURE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The wallet signature does not verify.<br />**Do:** Sign the prepared order with its funding wallet again.

<span id="SIGNATURE_NOT_ACCEPTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNATURE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ACCEPTED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This quote trades on a venue account and takes no wallet signature.<br />**Do:** Remove the signature.

<span id="SIGNATURE_TOO_LONG" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNATURE_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />LONG</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The signature is longer than allowed.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="SIGNED_NOT_FORWARDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNED_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FORWARDED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet answered the venue acceptance, but the trade was not claimed, so the signature was never sent to the venue; nothing traded.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SIGNED_OUTPUT_BOUND_UNATTACHABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNED_<wbr />OUTPUT_<wbr />BOUND_<wbr />UNATTACHABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The signed minimum receive cannot be attached to its wallet action.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SIGNED_OUTPUT_BOUND_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNED_<wbr />OUTPUT_<wbr />BOUND_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The signed minimum receive cannot be read.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote before signing.

<span id="SIGNER_LACKS_RIGHT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNER_<wbr />LACKS_<wbr />RIGHT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet's signer lacks the right this trade needs; nothing was signed.<br />**Do:** Grant the signer the right, or choose another wallet.

<span id="SIGNER_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNER_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet's signer is not available; nothing was signed.<br />**Do:** Retry later, or choose another wallet.

<span id="SIGNING_GRANT_NOT_RESERVED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />GRANT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />RESERVED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This order never reserved room in the wallet's signing grant, so no step of it can be signed; nothing was signed or sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SIGNING_GRANT_OPERATION_CLOSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />GRANT_<wbr />OPERATION_<wbr />CLOSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This order is already finished (filled, unfilled or cancelled), so its signing grant admits no further step; nothing more was signed or sent.<br />**Do:** Read the order's status; to trade again, request a new quote.

<span id="SIGNING_GRANT_POLICY_CHECKING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />GRANT_<wbr />POLICY_<wbr />CHECKING</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The wallet's installed signing policy is not currently verified; no preparation or funds were sent.<br />**Do:** Wait for the policy check to finish (a workspace member can see its state on the wallet in the TetraFi app), then request a new quote.

<span id="SIGNING_GRANT_POLICY_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />GRANT_<wbr />POLICY_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The wallet's installed signing policy could not be read; no preparation or funds were sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote shortly; if it persists, a workspace member checks the wallet's signing grant in the TetraFi app.

<span id="SIGNING_PROVIDER_RATE_LIMITED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />PROVIDER_<wbr />RATE_<wbr />LIMITED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet's signing provider refused at its usage or rate limit before signing; nothing was signed.<br />**Do:** Try again later, or ask your workspace admin to raise the signing provider's limit.

<span id="SIGNING_STEP_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />STEP_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 410 · Retry: no<br />The step is past its signBy. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Read the order for how it ended; to trade again, request a new quote.

<span id="SIGNING_STEP_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />STEP_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The signed transaction, transaction hash or signature does not answer the step; details.field names what differs (chainId, from, to, data, value, nonce, raw, txHash, signature, stepId or kind). Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Sign the step exactly as issued and send it again.

<span id="SIGNING_STEP_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />STEP_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />The order has no such step for this API key to sign.<br />**Do:** Use the stepId of the order's next.step, with the key that placed the order.

<span id="SIGNING_STEP_NOT_OPEN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />STEP_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />OPEN</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The step was already answered, or the order no longer waits for it. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Read the order; sign the step its next names, if any.

<span id="SIGNING_STEP_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />STEP_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The API key may not trade from this wallet now, or the trade's counterparty is no longer current; the message names the cause. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Fix the named cause (an admin restores the key's venue trading), then read the order.

<span id="SIGNING_STEP_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />STEP_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The order's firstStep does not fit: an order on a quote TetraFi signs takes none, and an order's firstStep is signedTransaction or signature (transactionHash and declined answer a step on the step route only; details.field is firstStep.kind). No order was placed.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="SIGNING_STEP_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />STEP_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The step could not be checked right now; details.cause names what could not be read (screening, chainRead, relay or stepStore). Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SIGNING_TRANSACTION_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />TRANSACTION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The network does not show the step's transaction hash yet. Nothing else was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SIGNING_TRANSACTION_REJECTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />TRANSACTION_<wbr />REJECTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The network refused the step's signed transaction when TetraFi broadcast it. When the refusal proves it was not taken (its fee, the wallet's gas money) nothing was sent and the step reopens with a new stepId at the same nonce; when its nonce is already taken, or a replacement was refused, the step keeps the transaction it holds and the chain decides it.<br />**Do:** Read the order and sign the step its next names.

<span id="SIGNING_TRANSACTION_TYPE_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />TRANSACTION_<wbr />TYPE_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The signed transaction is not an EIP-1559, EIP-2930 or legacy (EIP-155) transaction; blob (EIP-4844), set-code (EIP-7702) and pre-EIP-155 transactions are not relayed. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Sign the step as an EIP-1559 transaction and send it again.

<span id="SIGNING_WALLET_BUSY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />BUSY</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Another order is trading from this wallet on this network: an order holds the wallet it signs for its whole run, from its first step until it ends, because each step pins the wallet's next nonce. The nonce belongs to the address, so an order of another workspace holds it too when that workspace has proven the address or has signed a step from it; one that only names an unproven address never does. orderId names the holding order when it is your workspace's; an order of another workspace is never named. Two orders placed at once are told apart within seconds, the later one refused. A step's signed transaction or signature can also get this answer when it is handed in: if an order of another workspace signed from the same address first, it holds the wallet and the later hand-in is not relayed. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Answer or decline your order's open step, or wait for the other step to end, then order again.

<span id="SIGNING_WALLET_FUNDS_SHORT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />FUNDS_<wbr />SHORT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet no longer holds what the step spends, read on the network just now: details.field is balance (the token the trade spends) or gas (the network coin for the transaction's value and gas at its signed fee). Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Fund the wallet, then send the order or the step again; to trade a smaller amount, request a new quote.

<span id="SIGNING_WALLET_LIMIT_REACHED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />LIMIT_<wbr />REACHED</code> · 429 · Retry: yes<br />The API key (20) or the workspace (100) has named its allowance of new wallet addresses in the last 24 hours. Addresses already recorded still quote. No venue was asked.<br />**Do:** Name a wallet the workspace already holds, or try again later.

<span id="SIGNING_WALLET_NETWORK_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The wallet in source.tradingWallets is on a network no venue trades here, or a network whose wallets cannot sign their own trades yet (only EVM networks can). No venue was asked.<br />**Do:** Name a wallet on an EVM network TetraFi trades, or the wallet's id.

<span id="SIGNING_WALLET_NOT_ALLOWED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ALLOWED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This credential may not trade from a wallet it signs itself: only an API key or a signed-in member with the right to trade names one (a Viewer may not), or the workspace may not allow keys to trade from wallets it has not approved. No venue was asked.<br />**Do:** Use an API key or a member who may trade, ask an admin to allow it or approve the wallet, or name another wallet.

<span id="SIGNING_WALLET_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The wallet the client signs from did not pass screening. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Trade from another wallet.

<span id="SIGNING_WALLET_SCREENING_PENDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />SCREENING_<wbr />PENDING</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The screening of the wallet the client signs from is still running. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SIGNING_WALLET_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The wallet the client signs could not be recorded or linked right now, so no venue was asked.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SIGNING_WALLET_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SIGNING_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The wallet is one TetraFi signs for, and a trade the client signs from it itself is not available yet. No venue was asked.<br />**Do:** Name the wallet by its id with signer "tetrafi" to trade from it as TetraFi-signed, or name a wallet you sign yourself.

<span id="TRADER_SIGNATURE_NOT_APPLICABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TRADER_<wbr />SIGNATURE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />APPLICABLE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />Only certain continuations take a traderSignature.<br />**Do:** Remove traderSignature.

<span id="TRADER_SIGNATURE_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TRADER_<wbr />SIGNATURE_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The continuation needs the client's trader signature.<br />**Do:** Sign traderTypedData and send it as traderSignature.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Venues and accounts">

Connecting and reading venue and LP accounts, onboarding and company details. 120 codes.

<span id="ACCOUNT_DEPOSIT_HISTORY_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ACCOUNT_<wbr />DEPOSIT_<wbr />HISTORY_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as the reason of a 200 deposit-history read's unreachable entry, never as an HTTP status: this venue offers no deposit history to read.<br />**Do:** Confirm deposits at the venue itself.

<span id="ACCOUNT_NOT_AVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ACCOUNT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />AVAILABLE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The venue account the quote names is not available to this credential.<br />**Do:** Requote without that account, or use a credential that can trade on it.

<span id="ACCOUNT_READING_PAUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ACCOUNT_<wbr />READING_<wbr />PAUSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Reading this account is paused.<br />**Do:** Resume account reads in its Controls before reading instructions.

<span id="ACTIVATION_ITEM_OUTSTANDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ACTIVATION_<wbr />ITEM_<wbr />OUTSTANDING</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />An active venue connection has an outstanding setup item; it remains active.<br />**Do:** Complete the named item in the venue connection's activation checklist.

<span id="AGENT_VENUE_TRADING_NOT_ELIGIBLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>AGENT_<wbr />VENUE_<wbr />TRADING_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ELIGIBLE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />An agent service account cannot trade venues: venue quotes, venue prices, venue routes and organisation-wallet orders refuse its key.<br />**Do:** Trade through a human-operated service account's venue-trading key, or as a member.

<span id="BASE_PROFILE_INCOMPLETE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BASE_<wbr />PROFILE_<wbr />INCOMPLETE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The company's base profile (legal name, country of incorporation, registration number, registered address) is incomplete, so an order it originates on a venue's own rails cannot be checked; details.capabilityBlockers names the missing fields in currentlyDue. Nothing was admitted or sent.<br />**Do:** Complete the company details in the TetraFi app under Entity profile, then place the order again.

<span id="COMPANY_IDENTITY_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPANY_<wbr />IDENTITY_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The company's compliance identity changed after the order was prepared.<br />**Do:** Review the order again and place a fresh one.

<span id="COMPANY_IDENTITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPANY_<wbr />IDENTITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The company's compliance identity could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="COUNTERPARTY_STANDING_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COUNTERPARTY_<wbr />STANDING_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The counterparty's compliance standing could not be recorded or confirmed just now; nothing changed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="EDD_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>EDD_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />Enhanced due-diligence data is required; details lists the missing fields.<br />**Do:** Provide the missing fields named in details, then place the order again.

<span id="INVENTORY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INVENTORY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's account inventory or balance observations could not be read; nothing was released.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="KYC_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>KYC_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The identity or company verification this order is checked against has expired; a source transaction may already be confirmed.<br />**Do:** Read the order and any source transaction before renewing the verification; do not send the source again.

<span id="KYC_NOT_VERIFIED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>KYC_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />VERIFIED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The identity or company verification this order is checked against is not complete; a source transaction may already be confirmed.<br />**Do:** Read the order and any source transaction before completing verification; do not send the source again.

<span id="LP_CREDENTIALS_INLINE_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LP_<wbr />CREDENTIALS_<wbr />INLINE_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />A workspace order carried its own LP credentials; the workspace's venue connection signs workspace orders. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Remove lpCredentials from the order and place it again.

<span id="LP_CREDIT_EXHAUSTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LP_<wbr />CREDIT_<wbr />EXHAUSTED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The liquidity provider has no available credit for this order.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount, or choose another quote.

<span id="LP_DEFERRED_SETTLEMENT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LP_<wbr />DEFERRED_<wbr />SETTLEMENT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />An LP workspace's taker trading settles on-chain; deferred (credit) settlement is not available to it, so nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Choose a quote that settles on chain.

<span id="LP_ENTITY_RETIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LP_<wbr />ENTITY_<wbr />RETIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The account belongs to a retired LP identity and cannot provide funding instructions.<br />**Do:** Reconnect the account using the current LP identity.

<span id="LP_ORDER_REJECTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LP_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />REJECTED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The liquidity provider rejected the order's terms.<br />**Do:** Review the order terms, or choose another quote.

<span id="LP_PAGE_IDENTITY_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LP_<wbr />PAGE_<wbr />IDENTITY_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />Two rows of the listing share a paging identity, so no consistent page could be built; nothing was released.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="LP_PREFUNDED_BALANCE_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LP_<wbr />PREFUNDED_<wbr />BALANCE_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />This liquidity provider needs a prefunded balance before it can quote.<br />**Do:** Deposit funds with the provider, then request quotes again.

<span id="LP_RELATIONSHIP_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LP_<wbr />RELATIONSHIP_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's relationship with the liquidity provider, or its counterparty selection, could not be read; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="LP_REQUIREMENT_FACT_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LP_<wbr />REQUIREMENT_<wbr />FACT_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The liquidity provider enforces a serving requirement on a fact recorded for this workspace that could not be read (for example a placeholder country); nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Check the fact in the workspace's company profile and correct it, or contact TetraFi support.

<span id="LP_REQUIREMENT_INFORMATION_NEEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LP_<wbr />REQUIREMENT_<wbr />INFORMATION_<wbr />NEEDED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The liquidity provider enforces a serving requirement on information this workspace has not recorded; nothing was sent. details.items names the onboarding items that supply it, and lpId the provider.<br />**Do:** Record the named items in the workspace's company profile, then request a new quote.

<span id="LP_WORKSPACE_LINK_MISSING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LP_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />LINK_<wbr />MISSING</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />This LP workspace has no recorded LP link, so its trades cannot be read.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="OBSERVATION_AUTHORITY_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>OBSERVATION_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The caller lacks current authority to read this.<br />**Do:** Use a credential with read access to this resource.

<span id="OBSERVATION_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>OBSERVATION_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The caller's current read authority could not be checked.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="ONBOARDED_VENUES_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ONBOARDED_<wbr />VENUES_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · Stream event · Retry: yes<br />Sent as the reason of a 200 asset list's unreachable entry (source venueEligibility), never as an HTTP status: which venues the workspace is onboarded at could not be read, so the list may be incomplete.<br />**Do:** Read the asset list again shortly.

<span id="PROFILE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PROFILE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The signed-in person's profile could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="TAKER_ADMISSION_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TAKER_<wbr />ADMISSION_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's taker admission could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="TAKER_ONBOARDING_INCOMPLETE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TAKER_<wbr />ONBOARDING_<wbr />INCOMPLETE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />TetraFi has turned off taker trading for this LP workspace.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support to turn taker trading back on.

<span id="TAKER_WORKSPACE_TYPE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TAKER_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />TYPE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This workspace type cannot request taker prices or quotes.<br />**Do:** Use a taker workspace, or finish taker onboarding.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_ELIGIBILITY_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />ELIGIBILITY_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />A venue account in the balances answer was revoked, removed or became unreadable while the answer was built; no balances were released.<br />**Do:** Read the workspace balances again; the account then appears in unavailable with its cause.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_ELIGIBILITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />ELIGIBILITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Whether a venue account's balances may still be read could not be confirmed; no balances were released.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_FUNDING_VENUE_DISABLED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />FUNDING_<wbr />VENUE_<wbr />DISABLED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Only a disabled venue holds the account's balance key, so no deposit details were released.<br />**Do:** Re-enable that venue in the account's Controls, then read again.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_FUNDS_IN_WALLETS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />FUNDS_<wbr />IN_<wbr />WALLETS</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This wallet-based venue keeps funds in its linked wallets, not a venue account.<br />**Do:** Read the linked trading wallets' balances with GET /api/v1/workspaces/&#123;workspaceId&#125;/balances instead.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_HAS_NO_KEY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />HAS_<wbr />NO_<wbr />KEY</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue account has no current funding key.<br />**Do:** Connect or rotate the account's key, then read again.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_INSUFFICIENT_AVAILABLE_FUNDS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />INSUFFICIENT_<wbr />AVAILABLE_<wbr />FUNDS</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The paying account or wallet holds too little that is not already held for other open orders; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Fund the account, or wait for its open orders to finish, then request a fresh quote.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_KEY_NOT_CONNECTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CONNECTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue account's funding key is not connected.<br />**Do:** Reconnect and verify the account's key.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_LEDGER_PENDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />LEDGER_<wbr />PENDING</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Not observed yet: the venue account has not finished setup, so its ledger has not been read.<br />**Do:** A member with manage_credentials follows the finishVenueAccountSetup fix on target.venueAccountId, then reads its ledger again.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_NOT_CONFIGURED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CONFIGURED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue account is not configured for this funding read.<br />**Do:** Complete the account's setup and verification.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />This workspace has no venue account with that id.<br />**Do:** Use an account id from this workspace's venue accounts.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_REVERIFY_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />REVERIFY_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />No current key holds this venue account's balance.<br />**Do:** Re-verify or rotate the account's key, then read its instructions again.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_REVOKED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />REVOKED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This venue account is disconnected.<br />**Do:** Reconnect the venue account before reading its funding instructions.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_SERVICE_TIMEOUT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />SERVICE_<wbr />TIMEOUT</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The venue-account funding read timed out.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />SERVICE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The venue-account funding service is unavailable.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_UNVERIFIED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />UNVERIFIED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue account is not verified with the venue.<br />**Do:** Verify the venue account, then request a new quote.

<span id="VENUE_ACCOUNT_VENUE_UNKNOWN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />VENUE_<wbr />UNKNOWN</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The account's venue is not recognised.<br />**Do:** Reconnect the account to a supported venue.

<span id="VENUE_ADAPTER_ERROR" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ADAPTER_<wbr />ERROR</code> · 502 · Retry: yes<br />The liquidity provider is temporarily unavailable.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_BALANCES_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />BALANCES_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The venue account's balance answer could not be built; no balances were released.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_BALANCE_OBSERVATION_CONTENDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />BALANCE_<wbr />OBSERVATION_<wbr />CONTENDED</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />Another read of the same venue account's balance was publishing at that moment, so this request could not use a current balance.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_CANNOT_FILL" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />CANNOT_<wbr />FILL</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />The chosen liquidity provider cannot fill this trade right now.<br />**Do:** Request new quotes, or choose another quote.

<span id="VENUE_CLIENT_ERROR" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />CLIENT_<wbr />ERROR</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The liquidity provider refused the order request; details.venueCode carries its own refusal code (for example AMOUNT_BELOW_MINIMUM) when it sent one, and details.reason the source reason it stands for (amountBelowMinimum), or venueRefusedUnlisted for a code outside that list.<br />**Do:** Fix what details.reason names, or request a new quote or choose another venue.

<span id="VENUE_CONNECTION_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />CONNECTION_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Current authority could not be confirmed at the deposit disclosure boundary; no instructions were released.<br />**Do:** Check that the caller still has wallet-view authority, then retry shortly.

<span id="VENUE_CONNECTION_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />CONNECTION_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The account's connection, key or controls changed during the read; no instructions were released.<br />**Do:** Read the current account, then request its instructions again.

<span id="VENUE_CONNECTION_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />CONNECTION_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The deposit selection or stored funding configuration cannot be used.<br />**Do:** Correct the asset, network or linked bank selection as the message says; re-verify the account if its configuration is invalid.

<span id="VENUE_CONNECTION_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />CONNECTION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />The account's venue connection could not be found in this workspace.<br />**Do:** Read the current venue account and use its current connection.

<span id="VENUE_CONNECTION_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />CONNECTION_<wbr />STORAGE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The venue account's current funding state could not be read or its disclosure audit could not be committed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_CONNECTION_WORKSPACE_TYPE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />CONNECTION_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />TYPE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This workspace type cannot read venue-account funding instructions.<br />**Do:** Use a Company or Broker workspace, or an LP workspace admitted as a taker.

<span id="VENUE_CREDENTIAL_ENVELOPE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />CREDENTIAL_<wbr />ENVELOPE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />The account's funding credential could not be verified or opened.<br />**Do:** Rotate the account's key; contact support if the failure persists.

<span id="VENUE_CREDENTIAL_KEY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />CREDENTIAL_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The service needed to open the account's funding credential is unavailable.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_CREDIT_HOLD_NOT_RESERVED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />CREDIT_<wbr />HOLD_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />RESERVED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The credit held for this firm price is no longer reserved (used or released); nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="VENUE_DAILY_CAP_EXCEEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />DAILY_<wbr />CAP_<wbr />EXCEEDED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The trade exceeds the venue's daily pilot limit; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Choose another venue or wait for the next day.

<span id="VENUE_DATA_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />DATA_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 422 · Retry: yes<br />The venues could not read their own balances or prices just now; details.reason names which (balanceDataUnavailable, priceDataUnavailable, marketDataUnavailable).<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_DEPOSIT_NOTICE_STATE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />DEPOSIT_<wbr />NOTICE_<wbr />STATE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The Step's current declaration could not be read.<br />**Do:** Read the payment again; absence is not established.
