<span id="VENUE_FIRM_QUOTE_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />FIRM_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />BUDGET_<wbr />EXHAUSTED</code> · 429 · Retry: yes<br />The declared venue quote-rate pool is spent; no new provider ask was sent.<br />**Do:** Wait for details.retryAfter or details.retryAfterMs, then deliberately retry; details.reason is tetrafi:declaredPool.

<span id="VENUE_FIRM_QUOTE_COUNT_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />FIRM_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />COUNT_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's daily firm-quote count could not be read; the price remains blocked and no new ask is sent.<br />**Do:** Try again to read the count before requesting a firm price.

<span id="VENUE_FIRM_QUOTE_DAILY_CAP_REACHED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />FIRM_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />DAILY_<wbr />CAP_<wbr />REACHED</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as a 200 answer's firm-quote warning code, never as an HTTP status: the venue's daily firm-quote limit is reached; used, limit and resetsAt give the count.<br />**Do:** Wait until resetsAt, or ask another venue.

<span id="VENUE_FIRM_QUOTE_DAILY_NEAR_CAP" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />FIRM_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />DAILY_<wbr />NEAR_<wbr />CAP</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as a 200 answer's firm-quote warning code, never as an HTTP status: the venue's firm quotes today have reached 80% of its daily limit; used, limit and resetsAt give the count.<br />**Do:** Expect firm quotes at this venue to stop at the limit until resetsAt.

<span id="VENUE_FIRM_QUOTE_DECLINED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />FIRM_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />DECLINED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue declined to give a firm price for this trade; nothing was signed or sent.<br />**Do:** Get a new price, or try another amount or venue.

<span id="VENUE_FIRM_QUOTE_LIMITED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />FIRM_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />LIMITED</code> · 429 · Retry: yes<br />This venue's firm-quote limit is reached: this caller's own share of the minute (details.scope principal, one API key or member session) or the workspace's total for the minute or the UTC day (details.scope workspace). details.limit is the cap that refused and details.resetsAt when it lifts.<br />**Do:** Wait for the Retry-After period, then retry; a principal-scope refusal does not hold back the workspace's other API keys or members.

<span id="VENUE_FUNDING_BALANCE_SHORT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />FUNDING_<wbr />BALANCE_<wbr />SHORT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The order chose funding: balance, but the quote's held credit decision draws credit: the venue balance is short by balanceShortfall.shortBy (balanceShortfall.asset); nothing was sent. details.cause is balanceShort.<br />**Do:** Order the same quote again with funding: credit, or lower the amount and request a new quote.

<span id="VENUE_FUNDING_CREDIT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />FUNDING_<wbr />CREDIT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The order chose funding: credit, but the quote holds no credit decision (details.cause names why); nothing was sent and the balance was not used instead.<br />**Do:** Order the same quote again with funding: balance, or refresh credit and request a new quote.

<span id="VENUE_HELD_QUOTE_NOT_ADOPTABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />HELD_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ADOPTABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The held firm price no longer matches this order (its account, revisions, principal or bounds), so it cannot be adopted; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="VENUE_IP_NOT_WHITELISTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />IP_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />WHITELISTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue has not approved this trading connection's network access; no trade was sent.<br />**Do:** Ask the workspace's venue administrator to finish the venue's network whitelist setup, then place the order again.

<span id="VENUE_KEY_DAILY_LIMIT_REACHED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />DAILY_<wbr />LIMIT_<wbr />REACHED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This order would take the venue key's orders today (UTC) past the daily trade limit its owner declared on the key (tradeLimitPerDay, US dollars); nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount, wait for the next UTC day, or raise the key's tradeLimitPerDay by rotating the key with a higher limit.

<span id="VENUE_KEY_FEE_CAP_EXCEEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />FEE_<wbr />CAP_<wbr />EXCEEDED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This order's expected venue fee is above the fee cap its owner declared on the venue key (feeCap, US dollars); nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount, or raise the key's feeCap by rotating the key with a higher cap.

<span id="VENUE_KEY_FEE_NOT_KNOWN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />FEE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />KNOWN</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue key declares a fee cap and this order's venue fee could not be established before sending; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote; if the venue never states its fee, remove the key's feeCap by rotating the key.

<span id="VENUE_KEY_IDENTITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />IDENTITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The key's venue-account identity could not be read; no identity decision was recorded.<br />**Do:** Retry verification once the venue's account identity read is available.

<span id="VENUE_KEY_IP_RESTRICTION_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />IP_<wbr />RESTRICTION_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The account's key does not have the required IP restriction.<br />**Do:** Apply the required IP restriction at the venue, then verify the key.

<span id="VENUE_KEY_LIMITS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />LIMITS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The venue key's declared trade limits or its day total could not be read; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_KEY_REFUSED_LIKELY_IP_WHITELIST" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />REFUSED_<wbr />LIKELY_<wbr />IP_<wbr />WHITELIST</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue refused this key; TetraFi's egress IPs may not be whitelisted.<br />**Do:** Whitelist TetraFi's current egress IPs on this key at the venue, check the key's permissions, then retry the operation.

<span id="VENUE_MINIMUM_ABOVE_TRADE_CAP" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />MINIMUM_<wbr />ABOVE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />CAP</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue's minimum order is above its Pilot per-trade cap; no amount within the cap can trade.<br />**Do:** Choose another venue; a cap change requires the operator's decision.

<span id="VENUE_NOT_AVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />AVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This venue is not available for the funding read.<br />**Do:** Use an available venue account.

<span id="VENUE_NOT_AVAILABLE_TO_CREDENTIAL" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />AVAILABLE_<wbr />TO_<wbr />CREDENTIAL</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This credential cannot ask this venue for a firm quote.<br />**Do:** Use a credential with Request quotes, or a member session.

<span id="VENUE_OPERATION_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />OPERATION_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The order does not match the venue operation it continues.<br />**Do:** Read the operation, or request a new quote.

<span id="VENUE_ORDER_PREPARATION_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />PREPARATION_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue order could not be built from this firm price; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="VENUE_PAYOUT_CAP_EXCEEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />PAYOUT_<wbr />CAP_<wbr />EXCEEDED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The payout exceeds the venue's pilot limit; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount.

<span id="VENUE_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />POLICY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />How this venue executes cannot be read right now; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_PREFUNDED_BALANCE_INSUFFICIENT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />PREFUNDED_<wbr />BALANCE_<wbr />INSUFFICIENT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue account's free cash after other commitments does not cover this prefunded trade; nothing was sent. details.maxAmount is the largest debit that fits now, an exact decimal in details.maxAmountAsset (the trade's debit asset), read from the same balance that refused.<br />**Do:** Reduce the debit to at most details.maxAmount and request a new quote.

<span id="VENUE_READ_AUTHORITY_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />READ_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The caller's authority to read the workspace's venue accounts ended during the read; nothing was released. reason authority_ended: the credential or session was revoked or expired, no longer carries treasury_read, or no longer meets the workspace's MFA requirement. reason permission_withdrawn: its principal lost ViewWallets, its workspace membership or taker admission.<br />**Do:** Use a current credential with treasury_read whose principal holds ViewWallets, then read again.

<span id="VENUE_REGISTRY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />REGISTRY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The venue registry needed for the funding read is unavailable.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_RFQ_BELOW_MINIMUM" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />RFQ_<wbr />BELOW_<wbr />MINIMUM</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The amount is below the venue's minimum; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Increase the amount and request a new quote.

<span id="VENUE_ROUTING_OFF" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ROUTING_<wbr />OFF</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Routing for this venue enablement is off, so its Basic quote is not placed. Nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Turn the venue enablement's routing on, then request a new quote.

<span id="VENUE_ROUTING_STATE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />ROUTING_<wbr />STATE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The venue enablement's routing state could not be read in time, so the Basic order is not placed. Nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_SENDER_NOT_WHITELISTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />SENDER_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />WHITELISTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue has not whitelisted the address this trade is sent from (the paying wallet, or the workspace's trading executor); nothing was signed or sent.<br />**Do:** Ask the workspace's venue administrator to finish the venue's onboarding so it whitelists that address, or pay from a wallet the venue has whitelisted, then get a new price.

<span id="VENUE_SEND_DEADLINE_UNDECLARED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />SEND_<wbr />DEADLINE_<wbr />UNDECLARED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This venue declares no send deadline for its firm prices, so the order cannot be bounded; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a quote from another venue.

<span id="VENUE_SIGNING_GRANT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />SIGNING_<wbr />GRANT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This wallet has no active trading grant for the venue details.venueId names; the previous grant may have expired or been stopped. Nothing was prepared or sent.<br />**Do:** A wallet owner sets up a trading grant for this wallet and that venue in the TetraFi app; then request a new quote.

<span id="VENUE_TEMPORARILY_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TEMPORARILY_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The liquidity provider refused this order for now (a timeout or rate limit); the refusal is not final.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_CAP_EXCEEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />CAP_<wbr />EXCEEDED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The trade is above this venue's per-trade limit during its pilot; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_INITIATOR_NOT_PERMITTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />INITIATOR_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />PERMITTED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The venue account refused this initiator: it is neither a current workspace member nor an active human-operated service account, with a current sponsor, acting through an unrevoked signing key that has venue trading switched on and the venue_trades_write scope. Nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Use a current member, or a current human-operated service account's venue-trading key, and the same key for the quote and the order.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_IP_ALLOWLIST_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />IP_<wbr />ALLOWLIST_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400, 403 · Retry: no<br />An API key with venue trading (the venue_trades_write scope) must have an IP allowlist naming its own networks: non-empty, with no entry wider than an IPv4 /8 or an IPv6 /16 (0.0.0.0/0 and ::/0 are refused). 400: a create or edit would leave such a key without one. 403: an existing such key without one is refused on every venue-trading request; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Add the addresses your trading server calls from to the key's ipAllowlist (for example 203.0.113.10/32), then retry.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_KEY_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The API key's venue-trading settings changed since the request was admitted.<br />**Do:** Retry so the request is admitted under the key's current settings.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_KEY_DISABLED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />DISABLED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The API key's venue-trading switch is off.<br />**Do:** Turn on venue trading for the key.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_KEY_NOT_CURRENT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CURRENT</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The API key is revoked or expired, or is no longer current for this workspace. Nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Use a current venue-trading key, or create a new one.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_KEY_SIGNING_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />SIGNING_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Request signing cannot be turned off on a key that can trade venues: venue trading accepts only signed requests. The key and its secret are unchanged.<br />**Do:** Keep signing on, or use a key without venue trading for unsigned requests.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_KEY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The API key's venue-trading settings could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_PAYOUT_DESTINATION_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />PAYOUT_<wbr />DESTINATION_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The payout destination changed after the trade was prepared.<br />**Do:** Prepare the trade again.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_PAYOUT_FEE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />PAYOUT_<wbr />FEE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The payout fee is not valid for the venue's precision.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_PREPARATION_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />PREPARATION_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />The venue trade could not be read or its outcome could not be recorded, possibly after the order reached the venue (details.placementEffect uncertain): an order may exist.<br />**Do:** Read the order back by its quote (GET /api/v1/orders/by-quote/&#123;quoteId&#125;) before sending another, and never resend it with a new Idempotency-Key; a retry with the same key answers IDEMPOTENCY_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_RECONFIRMATION_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />RECONFIRMATION_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This confirmation placed no order; the trade needs a fresh quote accepted.<br />**Do:** Request a fresh quote and accept it.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_REVIEW_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />REVIEW_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The trade's review expired; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Prepare the trade again for a new review.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_REVIEW_PENDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />REVIEW_<wbr />PENDING</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The trade waits for a human approval on its venue trade review; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Wait for a human member to approve the review, then confirm again.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_REVIEW_REJECTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />REVIEW_<wbr />REJECTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The trade's review was rejected; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Do not retry; prepare a different trade if needed.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_REVIEW_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />REVIEW_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Current policy needs a human review the trade was not prepared with; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote so the trade is prepared for review.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_SCOPE_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />SCOPE_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />Venue-account trading needs an API key with the venue_trades_write scope.<br />**Do:** Use a key with venue_trades_write, venue trading on and HMAC signing.

<span id="VENUE_TRADE_SIGNATURE_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />SIGNATURE_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />Venue-account trading with an API key needs HMAC request signing.<br />**Do:** Sign the request with X-Timestamp, X-Nonce and X-Signature.

<span id="VENUE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The chosen liquidity provider is temporarily unavailable.<br />**Do:** Retry shortly, or choose another quote.

<span id="VENUE_UNKNOWN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />UNKNOWN</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The venue is not known; details.value names it.<br />**Do:** Use a venue id from a quote or GET /prices.

<span id="VENUE_WITHDRAWAL_DESTINATION_NOT_WHITELISTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />WITHDRAWAL_<wbr />DESTINATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />WHITELISTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue refused a withdrawal destination that is not whitelisted.<br />**Do:** Whitelist this destination address at the venue, then retry the withdrawal.

<span id="VENUE_WORKSPACE_ACCOUNT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>VENUE_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Deposit instructions require the workspace's own venue account.<br />**Do:** Use a workspace-owned venue account.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Wallets and custody">

Organisation, personal and custody wallets, and which wallet a key may trade from. 80 codes.

<span id="API_KEY_ORGANISATION_WALLET_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>API_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />ORGANISATION_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The API key's organisation-wallet grants or signing state could not be read, so the answer was refused rather than shown empty (GET /api/v1/me).<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="API_KEY_ORGANISATION_WALLET_KEY_NOT_ELIGIBLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>API_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />ORGANISATION_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ELIGIBLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Only a member's own API key or a service-account key trades organisation wallets; a legacy workspace key, or another member's key, is refused. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Use your own member key or a human-operated service account's venue-trading key.

<span id="API_KEY_ORGANISATION_WALLET_NETWORK_NOT_GRANTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>API_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />ORGANISATION_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />GRANTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The API key's grant names the organisation wallet but not the network the trade is on. A grant records its networks when it is written; switching a network on for the wallet never extends a key's grant to it. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Trade on a network the key's grant names, or a workspace Owner or ManageCredentials holder names this network in the key's organisation-wallet grant (walletNetworks).

<span id="API_KEY_ORGANISATION_WALLET_NOT_GRANTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>API_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />ORGANISATION_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />GRANTED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This API key may not use that wallet or delivery address: the organisation wallet the request names is not in the key's grant, or the deliverTo address on POST /quotes is not an organisation wallet the key may use.<br />**Do:** Use an organisation wallet the key is granted, or manage destinations in the app: a workspace Owner or ManageCredentials holder can add the wallet to the key's grant.

<span id="API_KEY_ORGANISATION_WALLET_SCOPE_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>API_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />ORGANISATION_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />SCOPE_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The API key was not granted the organisation wallet the venue would use. A quote source answers unavailable with it; an order or requote on that source is refused.<br />**Do:** Grant the key that wallet, or bind a wallet it holds.

<span id="API_KEY_WALLET_CREATOR_NOT_A_PERSON" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>API_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />CREATOR_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />A_<wbr />PERSON</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />Only a member's own API key creates a wallet; a service-account key or a client-credentials token is not a person who can be named as the creator.<br />**Do:** Use a member's own API key, or create the wallet in the app.

<span id="API_KEY_WALLET_FACTORS_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>API_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />FACTORS_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />An API key's wallet request named sign-in methods or a wallet for an owner to approve with. The person chooses their own sign-in methods or wallet on TetraFi's page; a key never picks a person's phone, authenticator app, passkey, key or wallet. Nothing was created.<br />**Do:** Send each owner with an empty factors list and no walletId, and send the person to approvalUrl, where they choose their own.

<span id="API_KEY_WALLET_GRANT_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>API_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />GRANT_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />An API key's wallet request asked for a step-up proof, a trade grant or initial access terms. A key creates the wallet only; every person approves it on their own signing page, and grants are made in the app.<br />**Do:** Send the request without stepUpProof, tradeFromThisWallet and initialAccess, then grant access in the app.

<span id="AUTO_WITHDRAW_ROUTE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>AUTO_<wbr />WITHDRAW_<wbr />ROUTE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />Automatic withdrawal has no route for this order.<br />**Do:** Turn off automatic withdrawal for this order, or choose another settlement wallet.

<span id="AUTO_WITHDRAW_WALLET_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>AUTO_<wbr />WITHDRAW_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />Automatic withdrawal needs a settlement wallet.<br />**Do:** Select a settlement wallet, then place the order again.

<span id="CUSTODY_WALLET_BROWSER_ONLY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CUSTODY_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />BROWSER_<wbr />ONLY</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The selected wallet signs in the browser, so it cannot be used for server-side signing.<br />**Do:** Send the wallet's own signature instead, or choose a custody wallet.

<span id="CUSTODY_WALLET_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CUSTODY_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The custody wallet is not registered in this workspace.<br />**Do:** Choose a custody wallet registered in this workspace.

<span id="CUSTODY_WALLET_WRONG_WORKSPACE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CUSTODY_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />WRONG_<wbr />WORKSPACE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The custody wallet belongs to another workspace.<br />**Do:** Choose a custody wallet of this workspace.

<span id="DELEGATION_DESTINATION_UNPINNABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATION_<wbr />DESTINATION_<wbr />UNPINNABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />No wallet provider can limit this venue's fund calls to your own destination, so a delegated signing grant can't be set up for it and the venue was not asked. Nothing was prepared or sent.<br />**Do:** Request the route on another venue (a quote's refusal names the venues that can carry it), or trade this venue from a wallet that signs each trade itself.

<span id="DESTINATION_NOT_APPROVED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DESTINATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />APPROVED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />deliverTo is not an approved withdrawal destination of the venue account. A quote source answers unavailable with it; an order or requote on that source is refused.<br />**Do:** Approve the destination on the venue account, or deliver elsewhere.

<span id="ONCHAIN_MARKETS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ONCHAIN_<wbr />MARKETS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · Stream event · Retry: yes<br />Sent as the reason of a 200 asset list's unreachable entry (source venueEligibility), never as an HTTP status: an enabled on-chain venue's markets could not be read, so the list may be incomplete.<br />**Do:** Read the asset list again shortly.

<span id="ONCHAIN_WALLET_NOT_USABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ONCHAIN_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />USABLE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This quote is indicative because the API key cannot use the organisation wallet.<br />**Do:** Grant the key an organisation wallet before ordering, then request fresh quotes.

<span id="PERSONAL_TARGET_NOT_ALLOWED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PERSONAL_<wbr />TARGET_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ALLOWED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This credential named a member's personal account in source.tradingWallets; only that member's own session trades from it. No venue was asked.<br />**Do:** Name an organisation wallet the credential is granted.

<span id="TARGET_NOT_ALLOWED_FOR_SLOT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TARGET_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ALLOWED_<wbr />FOR_<wbr />SLOT</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The bound wallet cannot be used for that slot.<br />**Do:** Bind a wallet allowed for the slot the message names.

<span id="WALLET_ACCESS_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />ACCESS_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The caller's wallet access changed while the answer was built; nothing was released.<br />**Do:** Read again under the caller's current access.

<span id="WALLET_ACCESS_CONCURRENT_CHANGE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />ACCESS_<wbr />CONCURRENT_<wbr />CHANGE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Wallet, wallet policy, custody or provider-credential facts in the workspace changed while the answer was built, and the caller's own membership and roles did not; nothing was released. A change to the caller's own principal answers WALLET_ACCESS_CHANGED.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="WALLET_BODY_AMBIGUOUS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />BODY_<wbr />AMBIGUOUS</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The request mixes the fields of a wallet creation with those of another wallet request, so it is not clear what to do.<br />**Do:** Send only the wallet creation fields (connectionId, ownerGroup and the rest).

<span id="WALLET_BODY_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />BODY_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The wallet request could not be read as a wallet creation body.<br />**Do:** Correct the wallet request as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="WALLET_CAPABILITIES_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />CAPABILITIES_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The wallet connection could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="WALLET_COMPLIANCE_REVOKED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />COMPLIANCE_<wbr />REVOKED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />A wallet on this quote had its on-chain compliance revoked.<br />**Do:** Use another wallet.

<span id="WALLET_CONNECTION_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />CONNECTION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />connectionId names no wallet connection of this workspace.<br />**Do:** Name a connection the workspace has (platform-turnkey for the one TetraFi provides).

<span id="WALLET_CREATION_ABSENCE_UNPROVEN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />CREATION_<wbr />ABSENCE_<wbr />UNPROVEN</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Whether the provider holds this creation's wallet could not be proven yet, so it is not withdrawn.<br />**Do:** Read the creation again shortly, then withdraw it if it is still pending.

<span id="WALLET_CREATION_ACTIVITY_EXISTS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />CREATION_<wbr />ACTIVITY_<wbr />EXISTS</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The provider holds an activity of this creation, so it cannot be withdrawn as if nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Read the creation again shortly; it settles to active or failed.

<span id="WALLET_CREATION_CANCEL_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />CREATION_<wbr />CANCEL_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The creation could not be withdrawn now; nothing changed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="WALLET_CREATION_EXISTS_AT_PROVIDER" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />CREATION_<wbr />EXISTS_<wbr />AT_<wbr />PROVIDER</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The provider holds the wallet this creation made, so it is not withdrawn.<br />**Do:** Read the creation again; it becomes active once recorded.

<span id="WALLET_CREATION_GOOGLE_CREATOR_ONLY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />CREATION_<wbr />GOOGLE_<wbr />CREATOR_<wbr />ONLY</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />A Google factor can be chosen only by the person it belongs to, at creation.<br />**Do:** Choose another factor for this owner, or let them choose Google on their signing page.

<span id="WALLET_CREATION_MEMBERSHIP_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />CREATION_<wbr />MEMBERSHIP_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The owners' workspace membership could not be read, so nothing was created.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="WALLET_CREATION_MEMBER_UNKNOWN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />CREATION_<wbr />MEMBER_<wbr />UNKNOWN</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />An owner named is not a current member of the workspace.<br />**Do:** Invite the person to the workspace first, then name them.

<span id="WALLET_CREATION_NOT_CANCELLABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />CREATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CANCELLABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The creation is no longer pending or uncertain (for example it is already active, failed or withdrawn), or it changed while it was being withdrawn.<br />**Do:** Read the creation again; only a pending or uncertain one is withdrawn.

<span id="WALLET_CREATION_NOT_OFFERED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />CREATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />OFFERED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This wallet connection does not create wallets.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="WALLET_CREATION_PERSONAL_OWNER" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />CREATION_<wbr />PERSONAL_<wbr />OWNER</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />A personal wallet's owners are its creator's own factors only.<br />**Do:** Create an organisation wallet to name other owners.

<span id="WALLET_DEFAULTS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />DEFAULTS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The saved default wallets and accounts could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="WALLET_FACTOR_NOT_OFFERED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />FACTOR_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />OFFERED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet connection cannot register one of the factors chosen for an owner.<br />**Do:** Choose factors the connection offers.

<span id="WALLET_FACTOR_NOT_VERIFIED_IDENTITY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />FACTOR_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />VERIFIED_<wbr />IDENTITY</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />An email or Google factor names an address that is not one of the owner's verified sign-ins.<br />**Do:** Choose an address the owner has verified, or another factor.

<span id="WALLET_FAMILY_NOT_OFFERED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />FAMILY_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />OFFERED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet connection does not create wallets in this family.<br />**Do:** Choose a family the connection creates, or another connection.

<span id="WALLET_FAMILY_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />FAMILY_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet's stored family cannot be read, so no network can be checked for it. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Contact support with this code and the request id.

<span id="WALLET_GROUP_NOT_EXPRESSIBLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />GROUP_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />EXPRESSIBLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The provider cannot hold this owner group exactly (one approval per person, each person one owner there), and a weaker rule is never made.<br />**Do:** Simplify the owner group as the message says.

<span id="WALLET_IDENTITY_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />IDENTITY_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The funding wallet is not ownership-verified for the order's owner.<br />**Do:** Verify the wallet for the owner, or use a verified one.

<span id="WALLET_INITIAL_ACCESS_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />INITIAL_<wbr />ACCESS_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Who manages and sees the wallet cannot be set as sent.<br />**Do:** A person does this in the app; an API key cannot.

<span id="WALLET_LINKED_TO_DIFFERENT_ACCOUNT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />LINKED_<wbr />TO_<wbr />DIFFERENT_<wbr />ACCOUNT</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The signing wallet is linked to a different account.<br />**Do:** Sign with a wallet linked to this account.

<span id="WALLET_LINK_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />LINK_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The signing wallet must be linked and verified first.<br />**Do:** Link and verify the wallet, then place the order.

<span id="WALLET_MEMBER_CURVE_UNVERIFIED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />MEMBER_<wbr />CURVE_<wbr />UNVERIFIED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />A wallet named as an owner signs with a key the connection cannot verify.<br />**Do:** Name a wallet whose key the connection verifies, or a person instead.

<span id="WALLET_MEMBER_FACTORS_BELOW_MINIMUM" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />MEMBER_<wbr />FACTORS_<wbr />BELOW_<wbr />MINIMUM</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as a 200 creation read's memberWalletUnavailable, never as an HTTP status: the reader's wallet holds fewer factors than the owner group requires of each member, so it cannot approve as a member.<br />**Do:** Add factors to that wallet, or approve as a person with your own factors.

<span id="WALLET_MEMBER_KIND_NOT_OFFERED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />MEMBER_<wbr />KIND_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />OFFERED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet connection cannot make this kind of owner.<br />**Do:** Use an owner kind the connection offers.

<span id="WALLET_MEMBER_WALLET_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />MEMBER_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />A member wallet is named beside sign-in methods, with more than one approval, or for another person: only the person whose wallet it is names it, as their one approval.<br />**Do:** Name your own wallet as your only approval, or invite the person to pick theirs.

<span id="WALLET_MEMBER_WALLET_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />MEMBER_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · Stream event · Retry: yes<br />Sent as a 200 creation read's memberWalletUnavailable or memberWalletOffersUnavailable, never as an HTTP status: the reader's own wallets could not be read, which is not the same as having none.<br />**Do:** Read the creation again shortly.

<span id="WALLET_NAME_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NAME_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet's name is empty or longer than 120 characters.<br />**Do:** Name the wallet in 1 to 120 characters.

<span id="WALLET_NETWORKS_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NETWORKS_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />A network is named twice, or is not a CAIP-2 id of the wallet's family.<br />**Do:** Name each network once, as a CAIP-2 id of the wallet's family.

<span id="WALLET_NETWORKS_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NETWORKS_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The request names no network for the wallet.<br />**Do:** Name at least one network (a CAIP-2 id) of the wallet's family.

<span id="WALLET_NETWORKS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NETWORKS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The wallet's networks could not be read right now (or not within the quote's deadline), so whether it may trade on the network is unknown. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Try again shortly.

<span id="WALLET_NETWORKS_UNRECORDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NETWORKS_<wbr />UNRECORDED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet has no network record, so no network can be checked for it. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Contact support with this code and the request id.

<span id="WALLET_NETWORK_ENVIRONMENT_MIXED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />ENVIRONMENT_<wbr />MIXED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet has never recorded the network, and switching it on would mix mainnet and testnet networks on one wallet. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Choose a network of the wallet's environment, or another wallet (refusalFix chooseNetworkOrWallet).

<span id="WALLET_NETWORK_NOT_DEPLOYED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />DEPLOYED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet is a smart account (contract code on a network it records) and has no code on the network it would gain, so funds sent there could not be moved. The network was not switched on or recorded.<br />**Do:** Deploy the smart account on that network first, or choose another network (refusalFix chooseNetworkOrWallet).

<span id="WALLET_NETWORK_NOT_IN_FAMILY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />IN_<wbr />FAMILY</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The network is not of the wallet's family (an EVM wallet named on Solana, say). Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Name one of the wallet's networks, or another wallet (refusalFix chooseNetworkOrWallet).

<span id="WALLET_NETWORK_NOT_REPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />REPORTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet is a custody account whose custodian has not reported the network, so it can't trade there and the network can't be switched on for it. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Choose another network or wallet (refusalFix chooseNetworkOrWallet).

<span id="WALLET_NETWORK_OFF" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />OFF</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The network is switched off for the wallet. A quote, preflight or order that pays from or delivers to the wallet there is refused (details.field names source or deliverTo.network, details.network the network), and authority on it (the API key's grant, a signing grant) is suspended there. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Trade on a network switched on for the wallet, or switch this one back on (refusalFix switchNetworkOn): the grant is usable again unchanged, with no new approval.

<span id="WALLET_NETWORK_SCREENING_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />SCREENING_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The newest screening result of the workspace wallet on the payout's network did not clear, so the wallet cannot pay there. Nothing was recorded or sent.<br />**Do:** Contact support to review the screening result.

<span id="WALLET_NETWORK_UNKNOWN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />UNKNOWN</code> · 400, 409 · Retry: no<br />The trade names a wallet endpoint, or a wallet on a network, TetraFi cannot place (neither an exact network nor a Solana or Tron family), so no authority on it can be checked. A quote answers 400, an order 409; either answers 409 when the network it can't place is one the wallet records, and the message names it. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a quote whose endpoints are on a supported network, then order on it.

<span id="WALLET_NETWORK_UNSCREENED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />UNSCREENED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />A payout from a workspace wallet on a network other than its home network needs a clear screening result of the wallet on that network, and there is none yet (or the result is under review). A custody vault is not screened per network. Nothing was recorded or sent.<br />**Do:** Try again later, or pay on a network the wallet is cleared on; TetraFi screens each network a wallet is switched on for. Contact support if it stays.

<span id="WALLET_NONCE_PENDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NONCE_<wbr />PENDING</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as a 200 venue trade's notSentCause, never as an HTTP status: an earlier transaction from the paying wallet was still unconfirmed when this trade's send window closed, so nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Wait for the earlier transaction to confirm, then request a new quote.

<span id="WALLET_NOT_LINKED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />LINKED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The funding wallet is not linked to the order's owner.<br />**Do:** Link the wallet to the owner, or use a linked one.

<span id="WALLET_NOT_ON_NETWORK" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ON_<wbr />NETWORK</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The bound wallet or address is not an account on the network it was bound to.<br />**Do:** Bind a wallet registered on that network.

<span id="WALLET_ORDER_SENDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />SENDING</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Another order from this wallet was still in its approval and debit step, and this quote had too little life left to wait for it, so this order was refused before anything was held or sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="WALLET_OWNERS_READ_PENDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />OWNERS_<wbr />READ_<wbr />PENDING</code> · Stream event · Retry: yes<br />Sent as a 200 creation read's memberWalletUnavailable, never as an HTTP status: the owners of the reader's wallet have not been read yet, so it cannot approve as a member yet.<br />**Do:** Read the creation again shortly.

<span id="WALLET_OWNER_GROUP_EMPTY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />OWNER_<wbr />GROUP_<wbr />EMPTY</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The owner group names no member.<br />**Do:** Name at least one owner.

<span id="WALLET_OWNER_GROUP_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />OWNER_<wbr />GROUP_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The owner group cannot be used as sent.<br />**Do:** Correct the owner group as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="WALLET_OWNER_MATCH_NOT_KEPT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />OWNER_<wbr />MATCH_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />KEPT</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as a 200 creation read's ownerMatchesUnavailable, never as an HTTP status: TetraFi keeps no proof for this person (they proved before proofs were kept, left the owners, or the wallet was retired), so their owners are not matched.<br />**Do:** The person proves their factors again on their signing page.

<span id="WALLET_OWNER_MATCH_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />OWNER_<wbr />MATCH_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · Stream event · Retry: yes<br />Sent as a 200 creation read's ownerMatchesUnavailable, never as an HTTP status: this person's stored proofs could not be read, so their owners cannot be matched.<br />**Do:** Read the creation again shortly.

<span id="WALLET_OWNER_MEMBER_DUPLICATE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />OWNER_<wbr />MEMBER_<wbr />DUPLICATE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The owner group names the same person or wallet more than once.<br />**Do:** Name each owner once.

<span id="WALLET_OWNER_THRESHOLD_OUT_OF_RANGE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />OWNER_<wbr />THRESHOLD_<wbr />OUT_<wbr />OF_<wbr />RANGE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The approval threshold is below one or above the number of owners.<br />**Do:** Set a threshold from 1 to the number of owners.

<span id="WALLET_OWNER_THRESHOLD_UNREACHABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />OWNER_<wbr />THRESHOLD_<wbr />UNREACHABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The chosen factors can never reach the threshold: owners that share one sign-in at the provider count as one approval.<br />**Do:** Lower the threshold, or give each owner a factor of their own.

<span id="WALLET_PORT_NOT_IMPLEMENTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />PORT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />IMPLEMENTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This wallet connection does not create wallets yet.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="WALLET_REGISTRATION_AUTHORITY_LOST" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />REGISTRATION_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />LOST</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as a 200 creation read's registrationFailure, never as an HTTP status: the wallet's creator no longer holds the right to manage wallets, so the active wallet is not yet in the directory; registration is retried.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin to restore the creator's right to manage wallets; registration is retried while the wallet is unregistered.

<span id="WALLET_RULE_SHARED_PRINCIPAL" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />RULE_<wbr />SHARED_<wbr />PRINCIPAL</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Two of the factors counted toward the threshold are one sign-in at the provider, so they cannot count twice.<br />**Do:** Count such factors once, or lower the threshold.

<span id="WALLET_SIGNATURE_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WALLET_<wbr />SIGNATURE_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The order is missing the wallet signature the quote requires.<br />**Do:** Sign the quote's terms in the paying wallet and include the signature.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Funding, balances and treasury">

Funding shortfalls, pay-ins, deposits, withdrawals, bank and fiat legs, and fees. 50 codes.

<span id="BALANCE_LIST_QUERY_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BALANCE_<wbr />LIST_<wbr />QUERY_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The balances query has an unknown or malformed parameter, an empty or over-long asset or accountId, or an accountKind other than wallet, venueAccount or bankAccount.<br />**Do:** Use only cursor, limit, asset, accountKind and accountId with valid values, then send the request again.

<span id="BANK_ACCOUNTS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BANK_<wbr />ACCOUNTS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's bank accounts could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="BANK_ACCOUNT_KEY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BANK_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />KEY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The bank-account credential needed for receiving instructions is unavailable.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="BANK_DESTINATION_NOT_SCREENED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BANK_<wbr />DESTINATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />SCREENED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The payout's bank destination has not been screened.<br />**Do:** Screen the bank destination, then place the order again.

<span id="BANK_DESTINATION_SCREEN_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>BANK_<wbr />DESTINATION_<wbr />SCREEN_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The bank destination's screening could not be read; nothing was admitted.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="DEPOSIT_HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DEPOSIT_<wbr />HISTORY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · Stream event · Retry: yes<br />Sent as the reason of a 200 deposit-history read's unreachable entry, never as an HTTP status: no current deposit history could be read from the venue.<br />**Do:** Read the deposit history again shortly.

<span id="DEPOSIT_SCREENING_NETWORK_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DEPOSIT_<wbr />SCREENING_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />Wallet screening does not cover the sending network, so the order is refused.<br />**Do:** Send from a network that screening covers.

<span id="DEPOSIT_SCREENING_PENDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DEPOSIT_<wbr />SCREENING_<wbr />PENDING</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Screening of the sending address is still running.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="FEE_SCHEDULE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FEE_<wbr />SCHEDULE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's effective fee schedule could not be read; no partial schedule was released.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="FIAT_ARRIVAL_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIAT_<wbr />ARRIVAL_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The bank deposit's expected arrival could not be established.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="FIAT_BALANCE_INSUFFICIENT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIAT_<wbr />BALANCE_<wbr />INSUFFICIENT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue account's balance does not cover this bank trade; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Add funds or lower the amount, then request a new quote.

<span id="FIAT_BALANCE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIAT_<wbr />BALANCE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The venue account's balance could not be read; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="FIAT_BANK_ACCOUNT_UNBOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIAT_<wbr />BANK_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />UNBOUND</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The bank quote names no bank account bound to this workspace.<br />**Do:** Bind a bank account for this venue account, then request a new quote.

<span id="FIAT_CARD_ACCOUNT_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIAT_<wbr />CARD_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The bank quote's venue account could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="FIAT_CARD_NOT_A_BANK_CARD" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIAT_<wbr />CARD_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />A_<wbr />BANK_<wbr />CARD</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The quote is not a bank quote, so bank terms do not apply.<br />**Do:** Remove the bank terms from the order.

<span id="FIAT_DEPOSIT_FIRST_NEEDS_MEMBER" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIAT_<wbr />DEPOSIT_<wbr />FIRST_<wbr />NEEDS_<wbr />MEMBER</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />Deposit-first trades need a signed-in member to confirm; an API key cannot consent.<br />**Do:** Have a member confirm the trade in TetraFi.

<span id="FIAT_DEPOSIT_FIRST_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIAT_<wbr />DEPOSIT_<wbr />FIRST_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />Deposit first is not available for this trade.<br />**Do:** Pay from the venue account balance instead.

<span id="FIAT_FUNDING_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIAT_<wbr />FUNDING_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The funding chosen does not match how the venue funds this bank trade.<br />**Do:** Remove the funding choice, or match the venue's funding model.

<span id="FIAT_FUNDING_MODEL_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIAT_<wbr />FUNDING_<wbr />MODEL_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The venue's funding model for this trade is not supported yet; nothing was placed.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote.

<span id="FIAT_PAYOUT_FEE_UNVERIFIED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FIAT_<wbr />PAYOUT_<wbr />FEE_<wbr />UNVERIFIED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The bank payout fee could not be verified.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="FUNDING_AUTHORIZATION_TOO_LARGE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />AUTHORIZATION_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />LARGE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />funderPermit2 is larger than allowed.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="FUNDING_DEPOSIT_ALREADY_USED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />DEPOSIT_<wbr />ALREADY_<wbr />USED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Another order already names this deposit (details.orderId); one deposit funds one order until that order ends without a fill; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Deposit again and name the new payout, or omit fundingDepositId.

<span id="FUNDING_DEPOSIT_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />DEPOSIT_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The order's funding deposit ended without moving the money (details.runStatus is its Run status); nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Deposit again and name the new payout, or omit fundingDepositId.

<span id="FUNDING_DEPOSIT_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />DEPOSIT_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The order's fundingDepositId names a payout that does not fund this trade; nothing was sent. details.cause names the first fact that differs: destination (not a deposit into this card's venue account), source (not from a company wallet), asset (not the card's debit asset) or amount (less than the card's debit amount).<br />**Do:** Name the deposit that moved at least this card's debit amount, in its debit asset, from a company wallet into its venue account, or omit fundingDepositId.

<span id="FUNDING_DEPOSIT_NOT_CREDITED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />DEPOSIT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CREDITED</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The order's funding deposit has not moved the money in full yet (details.runStatus is its Run status: awaiting approval, in flight or partly done); nothing was sent. next is wait.<br />**Do:** Execute again once the deposit has completed (next.retryAfter), or omit fundingDepositId.

<span id="FUNDING_DEPOSIT_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />DEPOSIT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />The order's fundingDepositId names no payout in this workspace; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Send the payoutId that POST …/treasury/payouts returned for the deposit into this venue account, or omit fundingDepositId.

<span id="FUNDING_DEPOSIT_SOURCE_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />DEPOSIT_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />fundingDepositId applies only to a trade paid from the venue balance, and the order chose funding: credit or depositFirst; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Omit funding (or send balance) with fundingDepositId, or omit fundingDepositId.

<span id="FUNDING_DEPOSIT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />DEPOSIT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The order's funding deposit, its state or the card's debit amount could not be read; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Execute again shortly, or omit fundingDepositId.

<span id="FUNDING_INFORMATION_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />INFORMATION_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as the reason of a 200 deposit-history read's unreachable entry, never as an HTTP status: the venue's deposit history, or the key's permission to read it, expired before it could be shown.<br />**Do:** Verify the venue account's key again, then read the deposit history again.

<span id="FUNDING_NOT_ACCEPTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ACCEPTED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This quote is not paid from a venue account, so it takes no funding choice or funding deposit.<br />**Do:** Remove the funding and fundingDepositId fields.

<span id="FUNDING_NOT_AVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />AVAILABLE</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />No pending funding step is available to this caller for this order.<br />**Do:** Read the order's current state; terminal orders do not offer funding data. Check the order id and workspace if no order is visible.

<span id="FUNDING_PATH_UNREVIEWED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />PATH_<wbr />UNREVIEWED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This funding path for the route has not been reviewed for use.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote.

<span id="FUNDING_SHORTFALL" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />SHORTFALL</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue account holds less than the trade needs; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Fund the venue account, then request a new quote.

<span id="FUNDING_SOURCE_UNRESOLVED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />UNRESOLVED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The order's funding source (the wallet and network the next step spends from) could not be resolved before the step was sent; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="FUNDING_STAGE_DISABLED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>FUNDING_<wbr />STAGE_<wbr />DISABLED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Funding on this venue is not open; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Choose another venue.

<span id="PAY_IN_AMOUNT_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PAY_<wbr />IN_<wbr />AMOUNT_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · Stream event · Retry: no<br />Sent as a 200 obligation read's dueRefusal, never as an HTTP status: the on-time payment differs from the amount the obligation requires; payInDifference says by how much.<br />**Do:** Settle the difference with the counterparty; the obligation stays open until the agreed amount is recorded.

<span id="PAY_IN_LEGS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PAY_<wbr />IN_<wbr />LEGS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The pay-in leg could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the read.

<span id="PAY_IN_LEG_NOT_BANK_CURRENCY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PAY_<wbr />IN_<wbr />LEG_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />BANK_<wbr />CURRENCY</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The pay-in leg is paid in a digital asset, so it has no bank payment details.<br />**Do:** Read the deposit address with asset and network instead of payInLegId.

<span id="PAY_IN_LEG_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PAY_<wbr />IN_<wbr />LEG_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />No pay-in leg of this venue account has that id.<br />**Do:** Read the account summary and use a current pay-in window's legId.

<span id="PAY_IN_LEG_NOT_OPEN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PAY_<wbr />IN_<wbr />LEG_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />OPEN</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The pay-in is no longer waiting for funds, so no payment details were released.<br />**Do:** Read the account summary for its current pay-in windows.

<span id="PAY_IN_LEG_SELECTION_EXCLUSIVE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PAY_<wbr />IN_<wbr />LEG_<wbr />SELECTION_<wbr />EXCLUSIVE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />payInLegId was sent together with another deposit selector.<br />**Do:** Send payInLegId alone, or the asset, network and providerBankId selectors without it.

<span id="PAY_WALLET_SHORT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PAY_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />SHORT</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The paying wallet holds less than the trade needs; details names the shortfall.<br />**Do:** Top up the wallet or choose another one, then request a new quote.

<span id="PAY_WALLET_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PAY_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The paying wallet the quote held was removed.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="TREASURY_ACCOUNT_FROZEN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TREASURY_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />FROZEN</code> · 423 · Retry: no<br />The source account is paused; other workspace accounts remain available.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin to resume this account.

<span id="TREASURY_DISABLED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TREASURY_<wbr />DISABLED</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />Trade preparation is not available on this server.<br />**Do:** Choose a quote that does not trade on a venue account.

<span id="TREASURY_FREEZE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TREASURY_<wbr />FREEZE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Whether the workspace's Treasury is paused could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="TREASURY_FROZEN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TREASURY_<wbr />FROZEN</code> · 423 · Retry: no<br />The workspace's Treasury is paused, so money movements are refused.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin to resume Treasury.

<span id="WITHDRAWAL_FEE_EXCEEDS_AMOUNT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WITHDRAWAL_<wbr />FEE_<wbr />EXCEEDS_<wbr />AMOUNT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue's withdrawal fee is larger than the amount received. A quote source answers unavailable with it; an order or requote on that source is refused.<br />**Do:** Use a larger amount.

<span id="WITHDRAWAL_FEE_UNVERIFIED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WITHDRAWAL_<wbr />FEE_<wbr />UNVERIFIED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue's withdrawal fee could not be read, so the minimum receive cannot be set.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="WITHDRAWAL_NETWORK_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WITHDRAWAL_<wbr />NETWORK_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue has no withdrawal route for that network. A quote source answers unavailable with it; an order or requote on that source is refused.<br />**Do:** Deliver on a network the venue withdraws to.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Settlement, credit and trades">

Settlement windows and obligations, credit, the ledger and trade records. 105 codes.

<span id="CREDIT_CUTOVER_PENDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDIT_<wbr />CUTOVER_<wbr />PENDING</code> · 429 · Retry: yes<br />The facility is fenced until its reported accounting reset.<br />**Do:** Retry after the reset time in the refusal.

<span id="CREDIT_EXHAUSTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDIT_<wbr />EXHAUSTED</code> · 429 · Retry: no<br />The order exceeds the workspace's remaining intraday credit.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount, or wait for the accounting reset.

<span id="CREDIT_IDENTITY_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDIT_<wbr />IDENTITY_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The order has no valid authenticated quote identity.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="CREDIT_LIMIT_BREACH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDIT_<wbr />LIMIT_<wbr />BREACH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The order needs more credit than the venue account has left under its credit limit; nothing was sent. details.creditRefusal.fix says what passes: reduceAmount &#123;maxAmount, asset&#125; when a smaller order fits, reduceExposure &#123;overBy, currency&#125; when the account is already over its limit and only a trade that reduces what it owes is admitted.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount to fix.maxAmount, or under reduceExposure place only a trade that reduces what the account owes, then request a new quote.
