<span id="CREDIT_READ_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDIT_<wbr />READ_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The venue account's credit could not be read fresh enough to check this order; nothing was sent. On a ledger read, details.component names the refusing source: credential (the key or account controls changed), positions, credit, depositInstructions, transferHistory, transferStatus or withdrawal; details.lastGoodReadAt names the last good read when known. The venue account item's ledgerReading carries the same code and component for a changed credential while retaining its last-good value and observedAt.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="CREDIT_RESERVATION_ACTIVE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDIT_<wbr />RESERVATION_<wbr />ACTIVE</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />This order already owns an active credit reservation.<br />**Do:** Resume the original order instead of creating another attempt.

<span id="CREDIT_RESERVATION_AMOUNT_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDIT_<wbr />RESERVATION_<wbr />AMOUNT_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This order's credit reservation has a different amount.<br />**Do:** Resume the original order with its unchanged amount.

<span id="CREDIT_RESERVATION_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDIT_<wbr />RESERVATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />The credit reservation could not be found.<br />**Do:** Read the original order before retrying.

<span id="CREDIT_RESERVATION_OWNERSHIP_LOST" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDIT_<wbr />RESERVATION_<wbr />OWNERSHIP_<wbr />LOST</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />This execution's credit preparation lease expired or was taken over; it may not dispatch.<br />**Do:** Read the original order, then retry the same order identity after its active attempt completes.

<span id="CREDIT_STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDIT_<wbr />STORAGE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Credit storage is unavailable.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="CREDIT_TERMS_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDIT_<wbr />TERMS_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />The workspace has no credit facility.<br />**Do:** Ask the workspace owner to arrange a credit facility.

<span id="CREDIT_VALIDATION_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CREDIT_<wbr />VALIDATION_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The credit request is invalid.<br />**Do:** Correct the credit terms or amount named in the refusal.

<span id="DEFERRED_ACCOUNTING_NOT_CONFIGURED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DEFERRED_<wbr />ACCOUNTING_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CONFIGURED</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />Deferred accounting is not configured for this route.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote; this route cannot be traded until it is configured.

<span id="DEFERRED_LP_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DEFERRED_<wbr />LP_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 502 · Retry: no<br />The liquidity provider could not take this deferred order (unreachable, timed out or a fault on its side); whether it was placed is not established.<br />**Do:** Read the order before trying again; do not place it twice.

<span id="DEFERRED_QUOTE_TERMS_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DEFERRED_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />TERMS_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The quote's deferred settlement terms (its signed payload, settlement details or legs) cannot be used; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="DEFERRED_SETTLEMENT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DEFERRED_<wbr />SETTLEMENT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Deferred settlement is not available for this liquidity provider right now; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry shortly, or choose another quote.

<span id="DEFERRED_SETTLEMENT_WINDOW_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DEFERRED_<wbr />SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The quote's settlement window is not one deferred settlement accepts; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="DEFERRED_TRADE_INTENT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DEFERRED_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />INTENT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />A deferred order must include the signed trade intent returned with the quote.<br />**Do:** Send the trade intent from the quote with the wallet's signature.

<span id="LEDGER_ACCOUNT_FORBIDDEN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LEDGER_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />FORBIDDEN</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The caller may not view this venue account's ledger, or the account is not available in this workspace.<br />**Do:** Use a credential whose principal holds View wallets in this workspace, or read an account of this workspace.

<span id="LEDGER_DATA_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LEDGER_<wbr />DATA_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 502 · Retry: yes<br />The venue answered the ledger read with figures that could not be read as exact amounts; none are shown.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="LEDGER_NOT_OBSERVED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LEDGER_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />OBSERVED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />No ledger read is recorded for this venue account yet; an API key or OAuth client reads only recorded observations and never starts a venue read.<br />**Do:** A workspace member opens the venue account in the TetraFi app, which records a read; then read again.

<span id="LEDGER_READ_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LEDGER_<wbr />READ_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The venue account's ledger or its access could not be read just now; details.lastGoodReadAt names the last good read when one is known.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="LEDGER_SETUP_MISSING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LEDGER_<wbr />SETUP_<wbr />MISSING</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue account has no current key, or its connection needs setup, so its ledger cannot be read.<br />**Do:** A member with manage_credentials adds or verifies the account's key, then reads its ledger again.

<span id="LEDGER_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>LEDGER_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This venue has no account ledger read.<br />**Do:** Read the account's balances instead.

<span id="RECORDS_OUT_OF_SCOPE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>RECORDS_<wbr />OUT_<wbr />OF_<wbr />SCOPE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The order belongs to another member, and the caller cannot read other members' orders.<br />**Do:** Read your own orders, or use a credential with compliance view permission.

<span id="RUN_DISCLOSURE_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>RUN_<wbr />DISCLOSURE_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Whether your access shows the Run in detail could not be read, so its included entry was not read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />The venue account the delayed-settlement quote names no longer exists in this workspace.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_CAP_EXCEEDED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />CAP_<wbr />EXCEEDED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The order exceeds the workspace's per-settlement cap.<br />**Do:** Lower the order amount below the settlement cap.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_CONSEQUENCE_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />CONSEQUENCE_<wbr />POLICY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The window's late-payment policy could not be read, so overdue status is not stated.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_CONTINUITY_ANCHOR_STALE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />CONTINUITY_<wbr />ANCHOR_<wbr />STALE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />An obligation covered by the selected snapshot anchor changed afterwards; no debt was netted.<br />**Do:** Build a new cumulative snapshot from the current obligation amounts and unpaid residuals; do not agree the stale snapshot.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_CONTINUITY_ANCHOR_UNCOVERED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />CONTINUITY_<wbr />ANCHOR_<wbr />UNCOVERED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The selected snapshot anchor never covered an outstanding predecessor; no debt was netted.<br />**Do:** Build a new cumulative snapshot from a predecessor basis that proves every outstanding obligation; do not agree the unchanged uncovered snapshot.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_CONTINUITY_MOVEMENT_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />CONTINUITY_<wbr />MOVEMENT_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />Canonical movement evidence changed after the snapshot; no debt was netted.<br />**Do:** Build a new cumulative snapshot with current canonical movement revisions and finality, then agree that snapshot.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_CONTINUITY_MOVEMENT_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />CONTINUITY_<wbr />MOVEMENT_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The cumulative position does not count the same movements matched to an earlier obligation; no debt was netted.<br />**Do:** Reconcile the named movement identities in the account journal, then build a new cumulative snapshot.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_CONTINUITY_MOVEMENT_PROOF_MISSING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />CONTINUITY_<wbr />MOVEMENT_<wbr />PROOF_<wbr />MISSING</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />This snapshot has no pinned canonical movement event proof; no debt was netted.<br />**Do:** Build a new snapshot that records canonical movement event ids and revisions before agreeing.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_CONTINUITY_MOVEMENT_REVERSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />CONTINUITY_<wbr />MOVEMENT_<wbr />REVERSED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />A payment counted by this snapshot was reversed afterwards; no earlier debt was netted.<br />**Do:** Build a new cumulative snapshot that includes the movement reversal, then agree that new snapshot.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_CONTINUITY_RECEIPT_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />CONTINUITY_<wbr />RECEIPT_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The snapshot continuity receipt does not validate against its immutable statement; no earlier debt was netted.<br />**Do:** Build a new cumulative snapshot with a validated continuity receipt, then agree that new snapshot.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_CONTINUITY_RECEIPT_MISSING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />CONTINUITY_<wbr />RECEIPT_<wbr />MISSING</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />This legacy snapshot has no validated continuity receipt; no earlier debt was netted.<br />**Do:** Build a new cumulative snapshot that proves the account journal and matched movements, then agree that new snapshot.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_CORRELATION_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />CORRELATION_<wbr />POLICY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Who could grant the missing permission could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_CORRIDOR_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />CORRIDOR_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Settlement capabilities for the network pair could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_DEADLINE_UNCONFIRMED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />DEADLINE_<wbr />UNCONFIRMED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The venue has not confirmed the settlement deadline of the window this trade would settle in, so nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** A member must record the venue's confirmed deadline on the account's schedule request in the TetraFi app. Poll GET /api/v1/workspaces/&#123;workspaceId&#125;/venue-accounts/&#123;venueAccountId&#125;/settlement/schedule-selection for the quote's venue account until `nextWindow.completionAt` is present, then request a new quote.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_FACTS_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />FACTS_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />A failedReads entry: the account's settlement facts could not be projected; the last good ones, if any, are shown with lastGoodReadAt and nothing is proposed from them.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_FACTS_LAGGING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />FACTS_<wbr />LAGGING</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />A failedReads entry: the account's settlement facts are more than 30 seconds behind a recorded change; the last projected ones are shown and nothing is proposed from them.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_FACTS_PENDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />FACTS_<wbr />PENDING</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />A failedReads entry: the account's settlement facts are not projected yet (a new account, or a new projector version), so its windows are not shown.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_FACTS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />FACTS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />A failedReads entry: the account's stored settlement facts could not be read, so its windows are not shown.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_FACTS_UNREADABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />FACTS_<wbr />UNREADABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />A failedReads entry: the account's stored settlement facts could not be decoded; they are being projected again and its windows are not shown.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_LATE_PAYMENT_ALLOCATION_AMBIGUOUS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />LATE_<wbr />PAYMENT_<wbr />ALLOCATION_<wbr />AMBIGUOUS</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />A late payment could be applied to more than one overdue obligation, so overdue status is not stated.<br />**Do:** Resolve which obligation the payment settles, then read again.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_LP_CORRECTION_RECEIPT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />LP_<wbr />CORRECTION_<wbr />RECEIPT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />An LP agreement correction or its immutable receipt could not be verified; no position was inferred.<br />**Do:** Restore the named agreement receipt and journal evidence, then retry the read.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_LP_DELIVERY_AGREEMENT_RECEIPT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />LP_<wbr />DELIVERY_<wbr />AGREEMENT_<wbr />RECEIPT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />This window's agreement record could not be read, so the LP delivery deadline is unavailable.<br />**Do:** Restore the window's agreement receipt, then retry the read.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MATCHING_RULE_UNCONFIRMED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MATCHING_<wbr />RULE_<wbr />UNCONFIRMED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The account's matching rule is not confirmed, so no movement was matched to an obligation.<br />**Do:** Confirm the matching rule for this account, then read again.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MATCH_CURSOR_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MATCH_<wbr />CURSOR_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The matches cursor does not belong to this window.<br />**Do:** Start the listing again without a cursor.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MATCH_PAGE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MATCH_<wbr />PAGE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The matches limit is out of range.<br />**Do:** Send a limit in the documented range, or leave it out.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MATCH_PRICE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MATCH_<wbr />PRICE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />A failedReads entry: the price that values the window's matched movements could not be read, so its counterparty progress is not stated.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MATCH_READ_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MATCH_<wbr />READ_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The matched movements could not be read; nothing was changed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MOVEMENTS_ACCOUNT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MOVEMENTS_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The venue account could not be read for its movements.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MOVEMENTS_AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MOVEMENTS_<wbr />AUTHORITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The caller's current workspace authority could not be read; nothing was released.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MOVEMENTS_LP_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MOVEMENTS_<wbr />LP_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The LP's movement history could not be read; recorded movements are unchanged.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MOVEMENTS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MOVEMENTS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The canonical movement journal could not be read or written; no payment was applied.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MOVEMENTS_WALLET_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MOVEMENTS_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The recorded movements could not be read; nothing was changed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MOVEMENT_EVIDENCE_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MOVEMENT_<wbr />EVIDENCE_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />Retained movement journal evidence conflicts with its canonical finality events; no payment or continuity was inferred.<br />**Do:** Reconcile the named canonical movement evidence before building a new cumulative snapshot.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MOVEMENT_EVIDENCE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MOVEMENT_<wbr />EVIDENCE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />A recorded movement's evidence could not be used; nothing was inferred from it.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MOVEMENT_FINALITY_UNPROVEN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MOVEMENT_<wbr />FINALITY_<wbr />UNPROVEN</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />A retained allocated movement has no durable finality history; no continuity was inferred.<br />**Do:** Re-observe the canonical movement to establish finality evidence, then build a new cumulative snapshot.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MOVEMENT_LIMIT_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MOVEMENT_<wbr />LIMIT_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />limit is outside 1 to 100.<br />**Do:** Send a limit from 1 to 100, or leave it out.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_MOVEMENT_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />MOVEMENT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />This venue account has no movement, or no window for the windowId filter, with that id.<br />**Do:** List the account's movements or windows and use an id from that list.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />No settlement for this trade is visible in the workspace.<br />**Do:** Check the trade id, and that it belongs to the workspace in the path.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_OBLIGATION_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />OBLIGATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />This venue account has no settlement obligation with that id.<br />**Do:** List the account's obligations and use an id from that list.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_OBLIGATION_READ_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />OBLIGATION_<wbr />READ_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The settlement obligations could not be read; nothing was changed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_OVERDUE_STATE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />OVERDUE_<wbr />STATE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Overdue status could not be read; nothing was changed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_OWNER_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />OWNER_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The executing wallet is not the verified owner wallet of the order.<br />**Do:** Use the order owner's verified wallet.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_PERMISSION_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />PERMISSION_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The credential's member lacks the workspace's view_settlements permission for obligations.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin for view_settlements; details.fix names who can grant it when a request can be made.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_PLAN_ACCOUNTS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />PLAN_<wbr />ACCOUNTS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's venue accounts could not be read for the plan.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_PLAN_FILTER_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />PLAN_<wbr />FILTER_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />venueAccountId filters the taker plan only; the maker perspective takes no account filter.<br />**Do:** Leave venueAccountId out of a maker plan read.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_PLAN_HORIZON_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />PLAN_<wbr />HORIZON_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />horizonDays is outside 1 to 90.<br />**Do:** Send horizonDays from 1 to 90, or leave it out.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_PLAN_PERMISSION_FIX_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />PLAN_<wbr />PERMISSION_<wbr />FIX_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Who could grant the missing permission could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_PLAN_READ_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />PLAN_<wbr />READ_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The settlement plan's answer could not be assembled; nothing was changed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_PLAN_TOO_LARGE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />PLAN_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />LARGE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The horizon holds more settlement windows than one plan answers (512); the plan is never truncated. As one account's failedReads entry in a 200 plan: that account holds more than 512 stored windows, so its windows are not shown and the rest of the plan is unaffected.<br />**Do:** Send a shorter horizonDays, then read again; for an account's entry, contact support.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_PLAN_VIEW_PERMISSION_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />PLAN_<wbr />VIEW_<wbr />PERMISSION_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The credential's member lacks the workspace's view_settlements permission for the settlement plan.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin for view_settlements; details.fix names who can grant it when a request can be made.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_PREDECESSOR_CHANGED_AFTER_SNAPSHOT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />PREDECESSOR_<wbr />CHANGED_<wbr />AFTER_<wbr />SNAPSHOT</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />An earlier settlement window was paid, delivered or changed after this snapshot; no successor obligation was created.<br />**Do:** Continue in a newer cumulative settlement window whose snapshot includes the earlier payment, delivery or change; do not retry the unchanged older snapshot.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_SCHEDULE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />SCHEDULE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The venue account has no confirmed settlement schedule that can produce a window, so nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Confirm the account's settlement schedule, then request a new quote.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_SCHEDULE_READ_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />SCHEDULE_<wbr />READ_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The account's settlement schedules could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_SCHEDULE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />SCHEDULE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The account's published settlement schedule cannot be used.<br />**Do:** Ask the LP to publish a valid schedule, then read again.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_SNAPSHOT_READ_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />SNAPSHOT_<wbr />READ_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The settlement windows or statement could not be read; nothing was changed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_SOURCE_BALANCE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />BALANCE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />A source the plan needs could not be read; details.fix names the read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_STATEMENT_READ_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />STATEMENT_<wbr />READ_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The statement's review records could not be read; nothing was changed.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_TRADE_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />This venue account has no trade with the tradeId filter's id.<br />**Do:** Use a tradeId of a trade on this venue account, or leave the filter out.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_VIEW_PERMISSION_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />VIEW_<wbr />PERMISSION_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The credential's member lacks the workspace's view_settlements permission.<br />**Do:** Ask a workspace admin for view_settlements; details.fix names who can grant it when a request can be made.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_WALLET_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This route needs settlementWalletId.<br />**Do:** Include settlementWalletId.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_WALLET_UNVERIFIED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />UNVERIFIED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />settlementWalletId must be a verified owner wallet.<br />**Do:** Verify the wallet, or choose a verified one.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_WINDOW_ACCOUNT_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />ACCOUNT_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The quote's settlement window belongs to another venue account than the one trading it, so nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_WINDOW_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The settlement window assigned at the send is not the one the order consented to, so nothing was sent. `details.currentWindow` is the window assigned now; `details.consentedWindow` is the window the order consented to.<br />**Do:** Review `details.currentWindow`, then resend the same quote with `settlementWindowId` = `details.currentWindow.windowId`.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_WINDOW_DEPOSIT_FIRST_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />DEPOSIT_<wbr />FIRST_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />A credit trade settles in the account's settlement window, so it cannot trade deposit-first.<br />**Do:** Trade from the account balance.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_WINDOW_NOT_APPLICABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />APPLICABLE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />settlementWindowId was sent on a quote that settles immediately.<br />**Do:** Send the order without settlementWindowId.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_WINDOW_NOT_CONSENTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CONSENTED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />At the send this trade needed credit, so it settles in the account's settlement window, but its quote settled immediately and carried no window to consent to. An order on a quote that carries a settlement window consents to that window even without `settlementWindowId`.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_WINDOW_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />This venue account has no settlement window with that id.<br />**Do:** List the account's windows and use an id from that list.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_WINDOW_NOT_QUOTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />QUOTED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The delayed-settlement quote was held without its settlement window.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_WINDOW_TERMS_PENDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />TERMS_<wbr />PENDING</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />This trade uses credit, so it settles in the venue account's settlement window, and the venue has not confirmed when that window closes, so nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** A member must record the venue's answer on the account's schedule request in the TetraFi app. Poll GET /api/v1/workspaces/&#123;workspaceId&#125;/venue-accounts/&#123;venueAccountId&#125;/settlement/schedule-selection for the quote's venue account until `nextWindow` is present, then request a new quote.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_WINDOW_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WINDOW_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The settlement window could not be verified, so nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SETTLEMENT_WORKSPACE_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SETTLEMENT_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />The workspace in the path is not a workspace id.<br />**Do:** Use the workspace id the credential belongs to.

<span id="TRADE_CONSUMED_READ_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TRADE_<wbr />CONSUMED_<wbr />READ_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Whether the trade was already used could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="TRADE_EXPORT_INCOMPLETE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TRADE_<wbr />EXPORT_<wbr />INCOMPLETE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Some trades could not be read, so no export file was made; the JSON list still answers.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="TRADE_LEDGER_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TRADE_<wbr />LEDGER_<wbr />AUTHORIZATION_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The signed trade authorization no longer matches the quote.<br />**Do:** Refresh quotes and sign again.

<span id="TRADE_LEDGER_AUTHORIZATION_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TRADE_<wbr />LEDGER_<wbr />AUTHORIZATION_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This order needs tradeLedgerAuthorization.<br />**Do:** Include the signed tradeLedgerAuthorization.

<span id="TRADE_LEDGER_INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TRADE_<wbr />LEDGER_<wbr />INSUFFICIENT_<wbr />BALANCE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The on-chain trading account does not hold enough for this order.<br />**Do:** Deposit funds to the account, then place the order again.

<span id="TRADE_LEDGER_INTENT_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TRADE_<wbr />LEDGER_<wbr />INTENT_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The order payload is not a valid trade intent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="TRADE_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TRADE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />No trade with this id is visible in the workspace.<br />**Do:** Check the trade id, and that it belongs to the workspace in the path.

<span id="TRADE_PERIOD_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>TRADE_<wbr />PERIOD_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The from and to filters do not form a valid period.<br />**Do:** Send a from that is not after to, at most 366 days apart.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Webhooks">

Creating, testing and redelivering webhook endpoints. 13 codes.

<span id="WEBHOOK_AUTOMATION_AMOUNTS_NOT_READABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />AUTOMATION_<wbr />AMOUNTS_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />READABLE</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The credential may not see the amounts of every Automation in the workspace, so it cannot subscribe to automation events; nothing was saved.<br />**Do:** Subscribe with a member or service account that can read the activity of every account the workspace's Automations use, or leave out the treasury.automation events.

<span id="WEBHOOK_AUTOMATION_AMOUNTS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />AUTOMATION_<wbr />AMOUNTS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Whether the credential may see Automation amounts could not be read, so automation events were not subscribed; nothing was saved.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="WEBHOOK_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The webhook endpoint's current state does not allow this action; the message names why, for example a paused endpoint.<br />**Do:** Reactivate the endpoint or read its current state, then send the request again.

<span id="WEBHOOK_DELIVERY_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />DELIVERY_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />This webhook endpoint has no delivery with that id.<br />**Do:** Use a delivery id from this endpoint's delivery log.

<span id="WEBHOOK_EVENT_UNKNOWN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />EVENT_<wbr />UNKNOWN</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />An events entry is not an event TetraFi sends; details.acceptedKeys lists the events you can subscribe to.<br />**Do:** Subscribe only to events listed in details.acceptedKeys.

<span id="WEBHOOK_EXISTS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />EXISTS</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This workspace already has a webhook endpoint for that URL.<br />**Do:** Use the existing endpoint, or register a different URL.

<span id="WEBHOOK_LIMIT_REACHED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />LIMIT_<wbr />REACHED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The workspace already has the maximum number of webhook endpoints.<br />**Do:** Delete an endpoint you no longer use, then register again.

<span id="WEBHOOK_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />This workspace has no webhook endpoint with that id.<br />**Do:** Use an id from this workspace's webhook list.

<span id="WEBHOOK_PRINCIPAL_UNRESOLVED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />PRINCIPAL_<wbr />UNRESOLVED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />This credential cannot be recorded as the webhook's accountable principal.<br />**Do:** Register the webhook with a member session, a member's API key or a service account of this workspace.

<span id="WEBHOOK_REDELIVERY_NOT_AVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />REDELIVERY_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />AVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This delivery offers no redelivery (its redeliverAvailable is false): it was withheld because the endpoint's principal may not read that activity; reason names the recorded outcome. Nothing was queued.<br />**Do:** Grant the endpoint's principal the permission the event needs; later events are delivered, this one is not resent.

<span id="WEBHOOK_REDELIVERY_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />REDELIVERY_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This delivery cannot be sent again now: the endpoint is paused, the delivery is still pending or already being redelivered, or its payload is not retained.<br />**Do:** Read the delivery and the endpoint; reactivate a paused endpoint or wait for the pending attempt, then retry.

<span id="WEBHOOK_VERSION_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />VERSION_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 412 · Retry: no<br />The webhook changed since the supplied If-Match version; nothing changed.<br />**Do:** Read the current webhook, review the changes, then send its new ETag in If-Match.

<span id="WEBHOOK_VERSION_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WEBHOOK_<wbr />VERSION_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 428 · Retry: no<br />The webhook edit has no observed strong version; nothing changed.<br />**Do:** Read the webhook and send its ETag in If-Match.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Compliance">

Capabilities, jurisdiction, screening and compliance policy. 29 codes.

<span id="ADDRESS_SCREEN_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ADDRESS_<wbr />SCREEN_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The sanctions address screen for the sending network could not answer; nothing was admitted.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="CAPABILITIES_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CAPABILITIES_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's verification could not be read, or changed during the read; no capability snapshot was released.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="CAPABILITY_BLOCKED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CAPABILITY_<wbr />BLOCKED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The workspace cannot use this capability; each entry in details.capabilityBlockers names the state in its code (BASE_PROFILE_INCOMPLETE: company details missing, with the missing fields in currentlyDue; KYC_NOT_VERIFIED: a direct user's identity verification missing), the step that clears it in unlockStep, and where in its message.<br />**Do:** Clear each blocker in details.capabilityBlockers: company details (legal name, country of incorporation, registration number, registered address) and a direct user's identity verification (KYC) are completed in the TetraFi app, not through the API; the request then succeeds unchanged.

<span id="CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CAPABILITY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Whether the workspace can use this capability could not be read (for example BASE_PROFILE_UNREADABLE: its company details could not be read).<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_ATTESTATION_NOT_CONFIGURED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />ATTESTATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CONFIGURED</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />On-chain compliance attestation is not configured for this route.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote; this route cannot be traded until it is configured.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_CONTEXT_UNRESOLVED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />CONTEXT_<wbr />UNRESOLVED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The compliance re-check before sending the order's next step could not resolve its amount, network or sending address; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_CREDENTIAL_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />CREDENTIAL_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The compliance credential could not be published on-chain just now.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_DATA_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />DATA_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The workspace's compliance policy needs per-trade data this order did not include; details lists the missing fields.<br />**Do:** Add the missing fields named in details, then place the order again.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The order did not pass compliance checks; a source transaction may already be confirmed.<br />**Do:** Do not retry. Read the order or source transaction and contact your compliance team or TetraFi support with the requestId.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />IDENTITY_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The signed trade authorization names a compliance identity that is not the signing wallet's.<br />**Do:** Sign the authorization again with the wallet the quote was made for.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_MIRROR_PROPAGATING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />MIRROR_<wbr />PROPAGATING</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Compliance status is still propagating to a network; the update has been re-sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_POLICY_PREFLIGHT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />POLICY_<wbr />PREFLIGHT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The settlement compliance policy could not be checked.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_POLICY_REJECTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />POLICY_<wbr />REJECTED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The order was refused by the settlement compliance policy.<br />**Do:** Contact your compliance team with the requestId.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_READINESS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />READINESS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The wallet's compliance identity could not be prepared just now.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_RECHECK_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />RECHECK_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The compliance re-check before sending the order's next step could not be read or recorded; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_SERVICE_NOT_CONFIGURED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />SERVICE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CONFIGURED</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />The compliance attestation service is not configured for this route.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote; this route cannot be traded until it is configured.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_SETTLEMENT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />SETTLEMENT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />Compliant settlement contracts are not configured for this request.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote or route.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_SNAPSHOT_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />SNAPSHOT_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The current compliance snapshot could not be read; nothing was admitted or sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_SUBJECTS_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />SUBJECTS_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The parties to screen for this order could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_SUBJECT_UNRESOLVED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />SUBJECT_<wbr />UNRESOLVED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The compliance re-check before sending the order's next step could not resolve whose order it is; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_TARGET_NOT_ATTACHED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />TARGET_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ATTACHED</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />The settlement contract is not attached to the compliance service.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote.

<span id="COMPLIANCE_TARGET_UNBOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COMPLIANCE_<wbr />TARGET_<wbr />UNBOUND</code> · 503 · Retry: no<br />The settlement contract has no compliance policy.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote.

<span id="PLATFORM_JURISDICTION_BLOCKED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PLATFORM_<wbr />JURISDICTION_<wbr />BLOCKED</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />An applicable entity country is on the mandatory platform denylist; no catalogue, credential or quote dispatch occurs.<br />**Do:** Trading is unavailable in this jurisdiction under platform rules.

<span id="PLATFORM_JURISDICTION_COUNTRY_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PLATFORM_<wbr />JURISDICTION_<wbr />COUNTRY_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />A primary or applicable address country is missing; mandatory platform clearance was not established.<br />**Do:** Record the entity's primary and applicable address countries, then request quotes again.

<span id="PLATFORM_JURISDICTION_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PLATFORM_<wbr />JURISDICTION_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />An applicable country or its mandatory platform observation could not be read; no venue was contacted.<br />**Do:** Correct unrecognized entity country data or retry after the profile observation recovers.

<span id="PLATFORM_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PLATFORM_<wbr />POLICY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The platform compliance policy could not be confirmed current, so the request (an order, a value movement or a wallet setup step) was not admitted and nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SCREENING_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SCREENING_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The screening provider is unavailable.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="WORKFLOW_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKFLOW_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's compliance workflow could not be read; the order was not admitted.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="WORKSPACE_COMPLIANCE_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>WORKSPACE_<wbr />COMPLIANCE_<wbr />POLICY_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The workspace compliance policy could not be confirmed current, so the order was not admitted.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Availability">

TetraFi or an upstream provider could not answer: retry as each row says. 8 codes.

<span id="CLOCK_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CLOCK_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />A server clock read failed (the request time, the settlement clock or a signing deadline), so nothing was admitted, sent or read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="INTEGRITY_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INTEGRITY_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The quote's integrity check failed: it was changed after it was issued.<br />**Do:** Send the quote exactly as returned, or request a new one.

<span id="INTERNAL_ERROR" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>INTERNAL_<wbr />ERROR</code> · 500 · Retry: no<br />Something went wrong on TetraFi's side. details.reason names what failed when known: storageFailed, responseEncodingFailed, orderEncodingFailed, orderRecordDerivationFailed or userResolutionFailed.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="PLATFORM_PROVIDER_NOT_CONFIGURED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>PLATFORM_<wbr />PROVIDER_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CONFIGURED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet connection TetraFi provides is not set up on this deployment.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="REQUEST_TIMEOUT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>REQUEST_<wbr />TIMEOUT</code> · 408 · Retry: yes<br />The request timed out on the server.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SERVICE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The service is unavailable. details.reason names what is unavailable when known: storageUnavailable, tradeViewUnavailable, deferredStateUnavailable, statementTimedOut or transactionTimedOut (with retryAfter).<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>UPSTREAM_<wbr />TIMEOUT</code> · 504 · Retry: yes<br />An upstream service timed out.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>UPSTREAM_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 502 · Retry: yes<br />An upstream service is unavailable.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Other execution">

Route continuations, server-side source execution and escrow settlement, mostly Advanced, which is not open yet. 80 codes.

<span id="CONTINUATION_ACCEPTANCE_NOT_OFFERED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />ACCEPTANCE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />OFFERED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The signed acceptance does not answer the continuation's current offer.<br />**Do:** Prepare the continuation again for a fresh offer, then sign that offer.

<span id="CONTINUATION_ACCEPTED_BY_RETIRED_TRADER" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />ACCEPTED_<wbr />BY_<wbr />RETIRED_<wbr />TRADER</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The continuation's trade was accepted before the client signed as trader; it is held for recovery.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="CONTINUATION_ASSET_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />ASSET_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />A trade that finishes an order must spend the asset that order left, on the same network.<br />**Do:** Spend the asset and network the parent order left.

<span id="CONTINUATION_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The prepared continuation step has expired.<br />**Do:** Prepare the continuation again (POST the continuation path), then sign it.

<span id="CONTINUATION_FUNDER_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />FUNDER_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The continuation was signed or funded by a wallet other than the route's taker.<br />**Do:** Sign the continuation with the wallet that placed the order.

<span id="CONTINUATION_FUNDING_PENDING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />FUNDING_<wbr />PENDING</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />Funding for this continuation was submitted or is awaiting confirmation.<br />**Do:** Read the order's status; do not start a replacement trade.

<span id="CONTINUATION_IN_PROGRESS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />IN_<wbr />PROGRESS</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />Preparation or funding of this continuation is already in progress.<br />**Do:** Read the order's status, then retry preparation to resume the same trade.

<span id="CONTINUATION_NOT_ARRIVED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ARRIVED</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The funds the continuation needs have not arrived yet.<br />**Do:** Wait, then read the continuation again before signing.

<span id="CONTINUATION_NOT_CLAIMED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CLAIMED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The continuation has no claimed signature to relay.<br />**Do:** Prepare the continuation and sign it before submitting.

<span id="CONTINUATION_NOT_PREPARED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />PREPARED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />No continuation step is prepared for this order.<br />**Do:** POST to the continuation path to prepare it, then sign the result.

<span id="CONTINUATION_ORDER_ID_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />ID_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The executing venue returned an order identifier the continuation cannot use.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="CONTINUATION_PARENT_NOT_FINISHABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />PARENT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FINISHABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The named order has no funds waiting to be finished in a new trade.<br />**Do:** Name the order whose funds are waiting, or place a new order.

<span id="CONTINUATION_PREPARED_BY_RETIRED_TRADER" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />PREPARED_<wbr />BY_<wbr />RETIRED_<wbr />TRADER</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This continuation was prepared before the client signed as trader.<br />**Do:** Let it lapse, then prepare it again.

<span id="CONTINUATION_REQUIRES_WORKSPACE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />REQUIRES_<wbr />WORKSPACE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />An order that finishes an earlier order must be placed in that order's workspace.<br />**Do:** Send the request in the original order's workspace.

<span id="CONTINUATION_REVISION_REFUSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />REVISION_<wbr />REFUSED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The continuation cannot be revised in its current state.<br />**Do:** Read the order's nextAction, then prepare again.

<span id="CONTINUATION_STATE_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />STATE_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The continuation's state changed while the request ran.<br />**Do:** Read the order's nextAction before retrying.

<span id="CONTINUATION_TRADE_NOT_FUNDABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />TRADE_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FUNDABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The venue closed this trade before it was funded; nothing moved.<br />**Do:** Prepare the continuation again for a fresh trade.

<span id="CONTINUATION_WALLET_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>CONTINUATION_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />A trade that finishes an order must spend from the wallet that holds its funds.<br />**Do:** Spend from the wallet the parent order left its funds in.

<span id="COUNTERPARTY_ROUTER_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>COUNTERPARTY_<wbr />ROUTER_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The counterparty's settlement contract is not available on this network pair.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote.

<span id="DELEGATED_EXECUTION_REQUIRES_WORKSPACE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />EXECUTION_<wbr />REQUIRES_<wbr />WORKSPACE</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />Server-side execution of the source needs a workspace context.<br />**Do:** Send the request with the workspace's credential.

<span id="DELEGATED_EXECUTION_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />EXECUTION_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Server-side execution is not available for this order.<br />**Do:** Sign and send the source transaction yourself, or choose another quote.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_ALREADY_SUBMITTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />ALREADY_<wbr />SUBMITTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />This order's source transaction was already submitted.<br />**Do:** Read the order's state instead of submitting again.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_AUTHORIZATION_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />AUTHORIZATION_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />Server-side execution needs the wallet owner's authorization.<br />**Do:** Include the owner authorization the order preparation asked for.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_AUTHORIZATION_UNUSABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />AUTHORIZATION_<wbr />UNUSABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The retained owner authorization can no longer be used.<br />**Do:** Prepare the order again and authorize it again.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_CUSTODY_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />CUSTODY_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 501 · Retry: no<br />Server-side execution does not support this wallet's custody provider.<br />**Do:** Use a supported wallet, or sign the source transaction yourself.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_DEADLINE_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />DEADLINE_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The source authorization's deadline has passed.<br />**Do:** Prepare and authorize the order again.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_DEADLINE_TOO_CLOSE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />DEADLINE_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />CLOSE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The source authorization expires too soon to be executed safely.<br />**Do:** Authorize again with a later deadline.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_DEADLINE_TOO_FAR" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />DEADLINE_<wbr />TOO_<wbr />FAR</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The source authorization's deadline is further away than allowed.<br />**Do:** Authorize again with a nearer deadline.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_EMBEDDED_WALLET_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />EMBEDDED_<wbr />WALLET_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 502 · Retry: yes<br />The wallet provider failed to execute the source transaction.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_INSUFFICIENT_GAS" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />INSUFFICIENT_<wbr />GAS</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The executor does not have enough gas to send the source transaction now.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_OWNER_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />OWNER_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The source transaction does not belong to the wallet that was screened.<br />**Do:** Register the source transaction from the screened wallet, or prepare a new order.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_PLAN_MALFORMED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />PLAN_<wbr />MALFORMED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The submitted source plan does not match what the order prepared.<br />**Do:** Send the source plan exactly as the preparation returned it.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_QUEUE_TIMEOUT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />QUEUE_<wbr />TIMEOUT</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The source transaction could not be queued in time.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_REPLACEMENT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />REPLACEMENT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The earlier source attempt must be replaced before sending again.<br />**Do:** Prepare the order again to get a replacement, then sign it.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_SIGNATURE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />SIGNATURE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The source authorization's signature does not verify.<br />**Do:** Sign the source authorization again with the funding wallet.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_SIGNATURE_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />SIGNATURE_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The source authorization needs a signature.<br />**Do:** Include the funding wallet's signature.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_STATE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />STATE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The order's source submission record could not be read or written; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Server-side source execution is not available for this order now.<br />**Do:** Sign and send the source transaction yourself, or choose another quote.

<span id="DELEGATED_SOURCE_UNSUPPORTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DELEGATED_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />UNSUPPORTED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This source cannot be executed server-side.<br />**Do:** Sign and send the source transaction yourself.

<span id="DEPLOYMENT_CHANGED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>DEPLOYMENT_<wbr />CHANGED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The contract deployment the quote or source authorization was made against is no longer the active one; details.reason is escrowDeploymentChanged or sourceDeploymentChanged. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="ROUTER_POLICY_NOT_ATTACHED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ROUTER_<wbr />POLICY_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />ATTACHED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The workspace router on the source network has no compliance policy for this action yet, so its source transaction cannot execute.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote after the router's policies are published; re-signing the same order will not help.

<span id="SOLANA_DOMAIN_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />DOMAIN_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The Solana order's domain does not match the signed message.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_ORDER_DEADLINE_PASSED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />DEADLINE_<wbr />PASSED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The Solana order's deadlines have passed.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_ORDER_NOT_OPEN" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />OPEN</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The Solana transaction did not open the expected escrow order.<br />**Do:** Check the transaction, or request a new quote.

<span id="SOLANA_ORDER_RECORD_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />RECORD_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The Solana escrow order does not match the quoted order.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_ORIGIN_FUNDING_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />ORIGIN_<wbr />FUNDING_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The Solana order was not opened by the order's own user.<br />**Do:** Open the order from the order owner's wallet.

<span id="SOLANA_ORIGIN_NOT_CONFIRMED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />ORIGIN_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />CONFIRMED</code> · 422 · Retry: yes<br />The Solana origin transaction is not confirmed yet.<br />**Do:** Wait for confirmation, then submit again.

<span id="SOLANA_ORIGIN_ORDER_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />ORIGIN_<wbr />ORDER_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />svmOriginSubmission.orderId does not match the order.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="SOLANA_ORIGIN_SIGNATURE_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />ORIGIN_<wbr />SIGNATURE_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The order signature must equal the Solana origin transaction signature.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="SOLANA_ORIGIN_SUBMISSION_MALFORMED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />ORIGIN_<wbr />SUBMISSION_<wbr />MALFORMED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />svmOriginSubmission is malformed.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.
