<span id="SOLANA_ORIGIN_SUBMISSION_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />ORIGIN_<wbr />SUBMISSION_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />Solana orders need svmOriginSubmission for the confirmed escrow-open transaction.<br />**Do:** Include svmOriginSubmission.

<span id="SOLANA_ORIGIN_TRANSACTION_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />ORIGIN_<wbr />TRANSACTION_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The Solana origin transaction failed.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_ORIGIN_TRANSACTION_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />ORIGIN_<wbr />TRANSACTION_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The Solana origin transaction was not signed by the funding wallet.<br />**Do:** Submit the transaction the funding wallet signed.

<span id="SOLANA_REGISTRY_NOT_READY" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />REGISTRY_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />READY</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The Solana deployment is not fully configured.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote.

<span id="SOLANA_RPC_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />RPC_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The Solana transaction cannot be verified because no Solana node is configured.<br />**Do:** Choose another quote.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_AUTHORIZATION_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />AUTHORIZATION_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The Solana source permit is invalid.<br />**Do:** Sign the source permit again as prepared.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_EXECUTION_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />EXECUTION_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />Source execution is temporarily unavailable.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_LOOKUP_TABLE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />LOOKUP_<wbr />TABLE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The prepared source transaction's address lookup table cannot be used.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_PERMIT_CORRUPT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />PERMIT_<wbr />CORRUPT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The retained source authorization could not be decoded.<br />**Do:** Contact TetraFi support with the requestId; do not retry in a loop.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_PERMIT_EXPIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />PERMIT_<wbr />EXPIRED</code> · 410 · Retry: no<br />The source authorization expired.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_PERMIT_NOT_FOUND" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />PERMIT_<wbr />NOT_<wbr />FOUND</code> · 404 · Retry: no<br />The prepared source authorization was not found.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_PERMIT_OWNER_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />PERMIT_<wbr />OWNER_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 403 · Retry: no<br />The source authorization belongs to another wallet.<br />**Do:** Use the funding wallet's authorization.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_PERMIT_REQUIRED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />PERMIT_<wbr />REQUIRED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />This order requires the prepared source authorization.<br />**Do:** Include the source authorization from preparation.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_PLAN_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />PLAN_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The source transaction's plan is no longer usable.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_PROVIDER_EXECUTION_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />PROVIDER_<wbr />EXECUTION_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet provider could not execute the source transaction.<br />**Do:** Read the order before trying again.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_QUOTE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The source quote does not match its prepared authorization.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_QUOTE_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />QUOTE_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The Solana source authorization no longer matches its quote and route.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_REFERENCE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />REFERENCE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The source transaction reference is invalid.<br />**Do:** Correct the request body as the message says, then send it again.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_ROLE_DRIFT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />ROLE_<wbr />DRIFT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The source transaction's required authority changed.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_RPC_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />RPC_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 503 · Retry: yes<br />The source transaction's network could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_SIGNATURE_CONFLICT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />SIGNATURE_<wbr />CONFLICT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />Another signature is already retained for this authorization.<br />**Do:** Follow the existing source transaction.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_SIGNATURE_INVALID" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />SIGNATURE_<wbr />INVALID</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The Solana source signature does not verify.<br />**Do:** Sign the source permit again with the funding wallet.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_SIGNING_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />SIGNING_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The wallet could not sign the source transaction.<br />**Do:** Check the wallet's signing authority before trying again.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_SIMULATION_REJECTED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />SIMULATION_<wbr />REJECTED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The source transaction failed its pre-send simulation.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_STATE_DRIFT" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />STATE_<wbr />DRIFT</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The source transaction's prepared state changed.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_STATE_UNAVAILABLE" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />STATE_<wbr />UNAVAILABLE</code> · 409 · Retry: yes<br />The source transaction's retained state could not be read.<br />**Do:** Retry the same request shortly, with backoff.

<span id="SOLANA_SOURCE_TRANSACTION_FAILED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />SOURCE_<wbr />TRANSACTION_<wbr />FAILED</code> · 409 · Retry: no<br />The source transaction failed.<br />**Do:** Read the order before starting another trade.

<span id="SOLANA_TRANSACTION_MALFORMED" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />TRANSACTION_<wbr />MALFORMED</code> · 400 · Retry: no<br />The Solana order payload is malformed.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_WORKSPACE_ROUTER_MISMATCH" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />ROUTER_<wbr />MISMATCH</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The quoted Solana settlement contract is not the workspace's active one.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote, then place the order on it.

<span id="SOLANA_WORKSPACE_ROUTER_MISSING" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>SOLANA_<wbr />WORKSPACE_<wbr />ROUTER_<wbr />MISSING</code> · 422 · Retry: no<br />The Solana order needs an authenticated workspace with a settlement contract.<br />**Do:** Send the request with a workspace credential, or choose another quote.

</Accordion>

## Source reasons

A source reason says why one venue did not quote; the request as a whole still succeeded. Read the remedy before retrying.

<Accordion title="Every source reason">

<span id="accountFeeUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>accountFeeUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue account's fee could not be read.<br />**Do:** Read prices again shortly.

<span id="accountFundingReadUnverified" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>accountFundingReadUnverified</code> · Retry: no<br />The account's key has no current verified balance-read check, so the venue was not asked.<br />**Do:** Verify the venue account, then request quotes again.

<span id="accountSetupIncomplete" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>accountSetupIncomplete</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue account is still being set up.<br />**Do:** A workspace member completes the venue account's activation steps in the TetraFi app; then request quotes again.

<span id="admissionUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>admissionUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's taker admission could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request again shortly.

<span id="advancedAccountEligibilityUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>advancedAccountEligibilityUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />Whether the venue account allows Advanced trading could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="advancedAccountStateInconsistent" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>advancedAccountStateInconsistent</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue account's Advanced state read inconsistently.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="advancedConnectionDisabled" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>advancedConnectionDisabled</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue account's Advanced connection is disabled.<br />**Do:** Enable it, then request quotes again.

<span id="advancedConnectionNotConfigured" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>advancedConnectionNotConfigured</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue account has no Advanced connection.<br />**Do:** Configure the Advanced connection.

<span id="advancedConnectionPaused" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>advancedConnectionPaused</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue account's Advanced connection is paused.<br />**Do:** Resume it, then request quotes again.

<span id="advancedModeDisabled" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>advancedModeDisabled</code> · Retry: no<br />This platform has not enabled Advanced trading, so the venue was not asked.<br />**Do:** Use a Basic quote, or wait until Advanced trading is enabled.

<span id="advancedNotAvailableForAccount" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>advancedNotAvailableForAccount</code> · Retry: no<br />Advanced trading is not available for this venue account.<br />**Do:** Use Basic, or another venue.

<span id="advancedNotEnabledForAccount" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>advancedNotEnabledForAccount</code> · Retry: no<br />Advanced trading is not enabled on this venue account.<br />**Do:** Enable Advanced on the account.

<span id="advancedSettlementNeedsVerification" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>advancedSettlementNeedsVerification</code> · Retry: no<br />An Advanced quote settles through TetraFi's router and escrow contracts, which need TetraFi verification (the workspace's on-chain credential, or the member's when the order pays from their personal wallet); nobody on this request holds it, so the venue was not asked.<br />**Do:** Complete TetraFi verification to settle through TetraFi's contracts, or choose a Basic quote.

<span id="allowanceInsufficient" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>allowanceInsufficient</code> · Retry: no<br />The paying wallet has not approved the provider's delegate for this asset.<br />**Do:** Approve the delegate from the wallet, then request quotes again.

<span id="allowanceUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>allowanceUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The wallet's token allowance could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="amountAboveMaximum" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>amountAboveMaximum</code> · Retry: no<br />The amount is above what the venue takes.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount.

<span id="amountAssetNotAccepted" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>amountAssetNotAccepted</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue does not accept the amount's currency for this pair.<br />**Do:** Choose another venue.

<span id="amountBelowMinimum" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>amountBelowMinimum</code> · Retry: no<br />The amount is below what the venue takes.<br />**Do:** Raise the amount.

<span id="amountInAboveMaximum" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>amountInAboveMaximum</code> · Retry: no<br />The swap needs more of the paying asset than the maximum the request allowed.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote.

<span id="amountNotAccepted" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>amountNotAccepted</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue does not accept this amount.<br />**Do:** Try another amount.

<span id="amountOutBelowMinimum" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>amountOutBelowMinimum</code> · Retry: no<br />The swap would deliver less than the minimum the request allowed.<br />**Do:** Request a new quote.

<span id="amountPrecisionUnsupported" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>amountPrecisionUnsupported</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue could not price this amount at its precision.<br />**Do:** Try a rounder amount.

<span id="amountUnreadable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>amountUnreadable</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue's amounts could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again.

<span id="amountsUnreadable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>amountsUnreadable</code> · Retry: no<br />The route's amounts could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again.

<span id="apiKeyAdvancedWalletUnsupported" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>apiKeyAdvancedWalletUnsupported</code> · Retry: no<br />Advanced quotes settle through TetraFi's router and escrow, which an API key cannot use, so the venue was not asked.<br />**Do:** Use a Basic quote, or quote this venue in a member session.

<span id="apiKeyOrganisationWalletNotGranted" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>apiKeyOrganisationWalletNotGranted</code> · Retry: no<br />The organisation wallet the venue would use is not granted to this API key.<br />**Do:** A workspace owner adds the wallet to the key's organisation wallets, then request quotes again.

<span id="apiKeyOrganisationWalletScopeRequired" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>apiKeyOrganisationWalletScopeRequired</code> · Retry: no<br />The API key holds no grant for an organisation wallet, so the venue has no wallet to quote for.<br />**Do:** Grant the key an organisation wallet, or bind a workspace wallet in bind.wallets.

<span id="askFirst" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>askFirst</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue prices only when asked directly.<br />**Do:** Name the venue in filters.venues.include, or requote its card.

<span id="askLimitReached" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>askLimitReached</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue's ask limit for this workspace is reached; `fix` names when it reopens.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again after the time the fix names.

<span id="assetNotCarried" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>assetNotCarried</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue does not carry this asset on this route; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Choose another venue.

<span id="assetNotTrading" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>assetNotTrading</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue is not trading one of this pair's assets right now.<br />**Do:** Choose another venue, or request quotes again later.

<span id="assetUnsupported" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>assetUnsupported</code> · Retry: no<br />The indicative source does not support an asset in this pair.<br />**Do:** Choose another asset or venue.

<span id="authorityUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>authorityUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The caller's trading authority could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="automationNeverAsks" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>automationNeverAsks</code> · Retry: no<br />Automated callers do not trigger a live ask to this venue.<br />**Do:** Requote the card to ask it.

<span id="availabilityUnreadable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>availabilityUnreadable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue's availability could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="backgroundNeverAsks" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>backgroundNeverAsks</code> · Retry: no<br />A background request does not ask this venue live.<br />**Do:** Ask in a foreground request or requote.

<span id="balanceAvailabilityUnconfirmed" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>balanceAvailabilityUnconfirmed</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue has not confirmed whether its balance is available to trade.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again once the venue reports its balance.

<span id="balanceMeaningUnconfirmed" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>balanceMeaningUnconfirmed</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue has not confirmed whether its reported balance is available to trade.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again once the venue reports its balance.

<span id="balanceNotObserved" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>balanceNotObserved</code> · Retry: yes<br />The paying wallet's balance has not been observed yet.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="balanceNotPermitted" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>balanceNotPermitted</code> · Retry: no<br />The caller may not read the venue account's balance.<br />**Do:** Use a credential with permission to read the venue account's balance.

<span id="balanceUnreadable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>balanceUnreadable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The paying wallet's balance could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="bankDestinationUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>bankDestinationUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue account's registered bank accounts could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="basicVenueAccountsUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>basicVenueAccountsUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's venue accounts could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="belowVenueMinimum" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>belowVenueMinimum</code> · Retry: no<br />The requested amount is below the venue's verified minimum; detail names the native amount and asset.<br />**Do:** Raise the amount; this is not a Pilot cap conflict.

<span id="billingPaymentOverdue" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>billingPaymentOverdue</code> · Retry: no<br />The organisation's TetraFi bill is more than 30 days overdue, so no venue was asked for a new quote.<br />**Do:** A workspace member with billing access pays the overdue statement in the TetraFi app; then request quotes again.

<span id="bondAmountUnreadable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>bondAmountUnreadable</code> · Retry: no<br />The route's bond amount could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again.

<span id="bondAssetUnreadable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>bondAssetUnreadable</code> · Retry: no<br />The route's bond asset could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again.

<span id="quotePreparationFailed" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>quotePreparationFailed</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue quote could not be prepared and verified, for a cause with no named reason; `detail` carries its code. No quote request was sent.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again; if it repeats, contact support with the code and the quote request's id.

<span id="quoteReconfirmationRequired" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>quoteReconfirmationRequired</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue's price changed before it could be confirmed.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again.

<span id="quoteRejected" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>quoteRejected</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue declined this quote request.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again, or choose another venue.

<span id="quoteRejectedCreditLine" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>quoteRejectedCreditLine</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue declined the quote: the trade is over the workspace's credit line with this credit-only venue.<br />**Do:** Refresh the account's credit, lower the amount or reduce what is owed, then request quotes again.

<span id="quoteRejectedInsufficientBalance" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>quoteRejectedInsufficientBalance</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue refused the quote because the paying balance is too low.<br />**Do:** Fund the paying account or lower the amount, then request quotes again.

<span id="quoteValidationTimedOut" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>quoteValidationTimedOut</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue's quote request may have been sent, but its answer could not be validated within the quote request's time; no quote was offered.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again.

<span id="rateLimitCapacityExhausted" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>rateLimitCapacityExhausted</code> · Retry: yes<br />TetraFi is pacing requests to this venue account; the venue was not asked.<br />**Do:** Wait for details.retryAfter when given, otherwise a few seconds, then request again.

<span id="ratePacerCapacityExhausted" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>ratePacerCapacityExhausted</code> · Retry: yes<br />TetraFi is pacing requests to this venue account; the venue was not asked.<br />**Do:** Wait for details.retryAfter when given, otherwise a few seconds, then request again.

<span id="venueDailyLimitReached" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueDailyLimitReached</code> · Retry: yes<br />The provider has reached this asset's daily transfer limit; a zero limit means the asset cannot trade today.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again on a later day, or choose another provider.

<span id="venueDataStale" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueDataStale</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue could not read its own balances or prices just now.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="venueDeclined" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueDeclined</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue declined to quote.<br />**Do:** Choose another venue.

<span id="venueEnablementRechecking" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueEnablementRechecking</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue's readiness for this workspace is being re-checked.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="venueEnablementUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueEnablementUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's venue enablements could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="venueError" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueError</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue answered with its own error code while the quote was prepared; `detail` names the code. Nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Contact support with the venue's code and the quote request's id.

<span id="venueFeeBalanceInsufficient" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueFeeBalanceInsufficient</code> · Retry: yes<br />The provider cannot pay this book's protocol fee right now.<br />**Do:** Choose another provider, or request quotes again later.

<span id="venueJurisdictionExcluded" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueJurisdictionExcluded</code> · Retry: no<br />The LP excludes an applicable incorporation, nationality or address jurisdiction; it was not asked.<br />**Do:** Choose an LP that serves the entity's recorded jurisdictions.

<span id="venueKeyRefused" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueKeyRefused</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue refused this workspace's API key when it was asked; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Verify the key on the venue's connection page (enter it again if it was rotated or revoked), then request quotes again.

<span id="venueKeyRefusedLikelyIpWhitelist" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueKeyRefusedLikelyIpWhitelist</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue refused this key; TetraFi's egress IPs may not be whitelisted.<br />**Do:** Whitelist TetraFi's current egress IPs on this key at the venue, check its permissions, then retry.

<span id="venueListingTimedOut" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueListingTimedOut</code> · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's venue list could not be read in time.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again.

<span id="venueMaintenance" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueMaintenance</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue answered that it is in scheduled maintenance, so it gave no price.<br />**Do:** Request prices again once the venue's maintenance ends.

<span id="venueMarketDisabled" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueMarketDisabled</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue has this market switched off.<br />**Do:** Choose another pair or venue.

<span id="venueMarketOffline" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueMarketOffline</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue lists this pair but has it offline right now; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly, or choose another venue.

<span id="venueMarketsUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueMarketsUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue's market list could not be read yet.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="venueMinimumAboveTradeCap" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueMinimumAboveTradeCap</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue's minimum order is above its Pilot per-trade cap; detail names the native minimum, its USD value and the cap.<br />**Do:** Choose another venue; changing the amount cannot fit both limits.

<span id="venueNotConnected" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueNotConnected</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue's account is not connected for this workspace.<br />**Do:** Connect the venue account.

<span id="venueNotEnabled" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueNotEnabled</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue is not enabled on the selected trading source's venue account; `fix` names who can enable it.<br />**Do:** A credentials manager enables the venue, then request quotes again.

<span id="venueNotEnabledForWorkspace" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueNotEnabledForWorkspace</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue is not enabled for this workspace.<br />**Do:** Enable the venue for the workspace.

<span id="venueNotListed" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueNotListed</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue no longer quotes this pair.<br />**Do:** Request fresh quotes.

<span id="venueOutcomeIndeterminate" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueOutcomeIndeterminate</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue may have received the quote request, but no readable answer arrived; no quote was offered.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again.

<span id="venueParticipantUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueParticipantUnavailable</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue connection names no provider participant for this book, so it was not asked.<br />**Do:** Choose another venue, or ask the venue administrator to complete the connection.

<span id="venuePaused" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venuePaused</code> · Retry: yes<br />The provider has paused this book.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again after the provider resumes trading.

<span id="venuePoliciesUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venuePoliciesUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue quote policies could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="venuePrefundUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venuePrefundUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The provider cannot prefund this book's payout right now.<br />**Do:** Choose another book or provider, or request quotes again later.

<span id="venuePriceInvalid" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venuePriceInvalid</code> · Retry: yes<br />The provider could not establish a valid price for this asset.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again after the price source recovers.

<span id="venueQuoteAdmissionExpired" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueQuoteAdmissionExpired</code> · Retry: yes<br />The quote request's time ran out before it could be sent to the venue; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again.

<span id="venueQuoteFailed" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueQuoteFailed</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue could not quote this request.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="venueQuoteIntentMismatch" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueQuoteIntentMismatch</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue account or the request changed while it was being quoted.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again; if it repeats, verify the venue account.

<span id="venueQuoteUnreadable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueQuoteUnreadable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue's pool quote could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="venueQuotingDisabled" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueQuotingDisabled</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue has quoting switched off for this account.<br />**Do:** Ask the venue to switch quoting on for the account.

<span id="venueRateLimited" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueRateLimited</code> · Retry: yes<br />TetraFi's request allowance for this venue is used for now; no quote request was sent.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="venueReenableRequiresNewCredentials" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueReenableRequiresNewCredentials</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue can be turned back on only with a new key.<br />**Do:** Enter a new key for the venue account, then request quotes again.

<span id="venueRefusedUnlisted" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueRefusedUnlisted</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue refused with a code outside its documented list (details.venueCode carries it on an order refusal).<br />**Do:** Request again, or choose another venue.

<span id="venueRelationship" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueRelationship</code> · Retry: no<br />The workspace's relationship with this venue does not allow trading.<br />**Do:** Resolve the relationship with the venue.

<span id="venueRelationshipUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueRelationshipUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The workspace's relationship with this venue could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="venueResponseUnmapped" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueResponseUnmapped</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue answered with a refusal that has no documented cause; no quote was offered.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again, or choose another venue.

<span id="venueRoutingOff" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueRoutingOff</code> · Retry: no<br />Order routing to this venue is off.<br />**Do:** Choose another venue.

<span id="venueRoutingStateUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueRoutingStateUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />Whether order routing to this venue is on could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="venueSiteUnqualified" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueSiteUnqualified</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue's market source has no qualified site for this deployment, so it publishes no price here.<br />**Do:** Contact support with the request id.

<span id="venueTradeCapExceeded" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueTradeCapExceeded</code> · Retry: no<br />The amount is above the venue's pilot trade cap.<br />**Do:** Lower the amount.

<span id="venueTradeKeyUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueTradeKeyUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The API key's venue-trading settings could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="venueTradeScopeRequired" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueTradeScopeRequired</code> · Retry: no<br />The API key can read indicative prices but cannot order without venue-trading authority.<br />**Do:** Re-issue the key with two-factor authentication, venue trading on and HMAC signing.

<span id="venueTradeUnreadable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueTradeUnreadable</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue's trade terms could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again.

<span id="venueTradingPaused" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueTradingPaused</code> · Retry: no<br />Trading on this venue account is paused, in TetraFi or by the venue, so it gave no price.<br />**Do:** Resume trading on the venue account in TetraFi, or contact the venue if it paused it; then request quotes again.

<span id="venueUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The venue could not price this request right now.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="venueWithdrawalDestinationNotWhitelisted" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>venueWithdrawalDestinationNotWhitelisted</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue refused a withdrawal destination that is not whitelisted.<br />**Do:** Whitelist this destination address at the venue, then retry the withdrawal.

<span id="walletAccountMissing" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletAccountMissing</code> · Retry: no<br />The wallet you named has no trading account record, so no venue was asked.<br />**Do:** Add the wallet in Treasury again or name another wallet, then request quotes again.

<span id="walletBalanceInsufficient" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletBalanceInsufficient</code> · Retry: no<br />The paying wallet does not hold enough of the paying asset for this swap.<br />**Do:** Fund the wallet or lower the amount, then request quotes again.

<span id="walletBlacklisted" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletBlacklisted</code> · Retry: no<br />The protocol or provider has blacklisted this wallet.<br />**Do:** Choose another wallet.

<span id="walletBrowserSigningEvmOnly" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletBrowserSigningEvmOnly</code> · Retry: no<br />One side of the transfer is a wallet signed in the browser on a network other than EVM; browser signing sends EVM transactions only. Nothing was asked or sent.<br />**Do:** Choose an organisation wallet for that side in the venue's activation (its Wallets step), then request quotes again.

<span id="walletBusy" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletBusy</code> · Retry: yes<br />Another order (of this workspace, or of another workspace that has proven the address or signed from it) is trading from the wallet the client signs on this network; the card still prices, and an order on it is refused SIGNING_WALLET_BUSY until that order ends.<br />**Do:** Answer or decline the other order's open step, or wait for it to end, then request quotes again.

<span id="walletFundsInsufficient" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletFundsInsufficient</code> · Retry: no<br />The paying wallet lacks the paying token or the network coin for transaction and account-creation costs; nothing was sent.<br />**Do:** Fund the wallet, then request quotes again.

<span id="walletGateUnreadable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletGateUnreadable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The paying wallet's trading eligibility could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="walletHasNoAddress" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletHasNoAddress</code> · Retry: no<br />The bound wallet has no address on this network.<br />**Do:** Bind a wallet with an address on the network.

<span id="walletNetworkMismatch" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletNetworkMismatch</code> · Retry: no<br />The saved wallet is registered on a different network.<br />**Do:** Choose a wallet on this network.

<span id="walletNetworks" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletNetworks</code> · Retry: yes<br />The networks switched on for the wallet you named could not be read right now, so no venue was asked.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="walletNotOnNetwork" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletNotOnNetwork</code> · Retry: no<br />The chosen wallet is registered on another network, so the venue was not asked.<br />**Do:** Choose a wallet on this network.

<span id="walletNotUsableByYou" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletNotUsableByYou</code> · Retry: no<br />The linked wallet is visible to this member but cannot be used by them for trading.<br />**Do:** A workspace admin gives the member rights to the wallet in the TetraFi app, or choose a wallet they may use.

<span id="walletNotVerifiedForAce" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletNotVerifiedForAce</code> · Retry: no<br />The wallet has no on-chain compliance identity, so the quote was withheld.<br />**Do:** Verify the wallet, then request quotes again.

<span id="walletNotWhitelisted" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletNotWhitelisted</code> · Retry: no<br />The saved wallet is not approved for this venue account.<br />**Do:** Approve the wallet on the venue account, or choose another.

<span id="walletNotWhitelistedForAsset" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletNotWhitelistedForAsset</code> · Retry: no<br />The paying wallet is not approved by the asset's issuer for this asset.<br />**Do:** Choose an issuer-approved wallet, or complete the asset issuer's onboarding.

<span id="walletPolicyUnsupported" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletPolicyUnsupported</code> · Retry: no<br />The wallet provider cannot limit this wallet's signing to this book's calls.<br />**Do:** Choose another wallet.

<span id="walletQuorumTooSlow" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletQuorumTooSlow</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue needs a wallet that can sign the swap within about 20 s; this wallet needs more than one approver.<br />**Do:** Choose a wallet one person can sign for.

<span id="walletQuorumUnreadable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletQuorumUnreadable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The wallet's approval quorum could not be read, so the venue was not asked.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="walletRead" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletRead</code> · Retry: yes<br />The wallet you named could not be read right now, so no venue was asked.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly.

<span id="walletRefused" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletRefused</code> · Retry: no<br />The bound wallet cannot trade on this venue.<br />**Do:** Bind another wallet.

<span id="walletTokenAccountFrozen" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletTokenAccountFrozen</code> · Retry: no<br />The wallet's token account for this asset is frozen, so the asset cannot move.<br />**Do:** Ask the asset issuer to approve or unfreeze the wallet, or choose another wallet.

<span id="walletUnavailable" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>walletUnavailable</code> · Retry: yes<br />The bound wallet could not be used just now.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again shortly, or bind another wallet.

<span id="withdrawalFeeUnread" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>withdrawalFeeUnread</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue's withdrawal fee could not be read.<br />**Do:** Request quotes again.

<span id="withdrawalNetworkUnsupported" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>withdrawalNetworkUnsupported</code> · Retry: no<br />The venue has no withdrawal route for that network.<br />**Do:** Deliver on a network the venue withdraws to.

<span id="workspaceJurisdictionRequired" style={{ scrollMarginTop: '10rem' }}></span><code style={{ overflowWrap: 'anywhere', whiteSpace: 'normal' }}>workspaceJurisdictionRequired</code> · Retry: no<br />The entity's primary jurisdiction or required address country is missing; the venue was not asked.<br />**Do:** Complete the entity profile's primary jurisdiction and registered or residence address country.

</Accordion>
